### [63] [Queen-Bee Agents: A BeeSpec-Centered Architecture for Governed Enterprise MCP Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06545)
*Dutao Zhang,Liaotian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 Queen-Bee 架构——一种以 BeeSpec 中间表示为核心的受治理企业级 MCP 多智能体编排框架,通过将控制平面(Queen)与执行平面(Bee)解耦,实现租户隔离、策略强制和审计可追溯。在 59 个企业风格任务上,检索驱动的 Queen-Bee 变体取得 0.964 的任务成功率、零治理失败和近乎完美的作用域执行质量,显著优于静态基线、无策略变体和单智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业部署 LLM 智能体面临的核心问题不仅是任务能力,更包括策略执行、租户隔离和明确操作边界。现有单体智能体设计存在结构性缺陷:广泛能力往往与广泛特权耦合,容易越权访问 HR/财务/IT 边界,即使最终结果看似有用,操作上仍不可接受。论文主张企业更需要的是:能够配置专业化执行单元、分配有界能力、强制执行运行时策略约束的受治理编排系统。

Method: 1. 四层架构:(1) Queen 控制平面——负责能力检索、蓝图规划、BeeSpec 生成和治理决策;(2) BeeSpec 中间层——显式定义 Bee 的执行边界(身份、角色、域、租户/内存作用域、可用工具、策略配置、审批门);(3) Bee 执行平面——在 BeeSpec 约束下执行;(4) 租户范围 MCP 连接器层——在活跃租户内解析工具调用。2. 多种配置后端:轻量结构化检索、加噪压力检索、稀疏+稠密混合检索、LLM 引导配置。3. Python 原型实现:包含 HR/IT 域 Bee、租户作用域 MCP 连接器、FastMCP stdio 适配器、策略引擎。4. 化学工作流集成:RDKit/ChEMBL/PubChem 真实软件与外部数据源。5. 评估 7 个系统变体在 59 个任务上的表现,涵盖治理、配置和作用域执行三方面。

Result: 1. 主对比:检索驱动 Queen-Bee 任务成功率 0.964,财务护栏拦截率 1.0,跨租户拦截率 1.0,租户范围准确率 1.0,仅有 1 次错误工具调用;而无策略和单智能体基线在治理指标上全部归零,任务成功率仅 0.571。2. 隔离与治理:所有受治理的 Queen-Bee 变体均实现完美拦截(1.0),证明治理贡献不依赖于专业化本身。3. 检索驱动配置:轻量结构化检索取得 0.979 的工具选择精度,8 个配置案例;混合检索和 LLM 引导配置可行但未超越轻量基线。4. 作用域 Bee 执行:检索驱动变体的执行完整率从静态版本的 0.80 提升至 0.95,工具正确率从 0.90 提升至 0.973。5. 化学工作流:在 TNIK 重新定位案例中,Evidence Bee、Screening Bee、Decision Bee 三阶段协作产出 Sunitinib、Pelitinib、Ruboxistaurin 的具体前 3 短名单,并支持审批门暂停执行。6. 效率:轻量检索端到端延迟 2.60 ms,显著低于混合检索的数十毫秒和 LLM 配置的数百毫秒。7. 错误分析:主要失败模式为"作用域任务过度执行",检索驱动版本将其从 4 例压缩至 1 例。

Conclusion: 1. 主要贡献:在小型、高度结构化的企业能力注册表下,受治理的轻量配置策略可同时实现更好的隔离、配置质量和执行质量。2. 核心启示:企业智能体平台应从单一"能力"维度扩展到多维评估——包括受治理配置、隔离行为、作用域执行质量和工件感知工作流协调。3. 架构普适性:BeeSpec 中心化设计从 HR/IT 域扩展到化学筛选工作流,证明其跨领域适用性。4. 设计哲学:在当前注册表规模下,更丰富的检索栈和模型驱动编排并不自动带来实际企业系统的提升;最显著的收益来自受治理专业化、显式能力结构和通过 BeeSpec 的作用域执行。5. 局限性:任务集为合成、采用本地演示 MCP 服务器、治理模型以规则为主、加噪测试为受控结构化扰动——这些是原型级系统证据,非生产部署研究或正式安全保障。

Abstract: Enterprise agent systems increasingly need to connect large language models to private tools, internal knowledge, and Model Context Protocol (MCP) interfaces. In this setting, raw task capability is insufficient: organizations also require policy enforcement, tenant-scoped isolation, and execution that remains within explicit operational boundaries. We present Queen-Bee, a governed multi-agent architecture in which a Queen control plane retrieves capabilities, plans task-scoped execution, and compiles a structured BeeSpec that is executed by specialized Bee agents under constrained tool access. We implement a working prototype with tenant-scoped MCP connectors, audit-backed execution-time governance, retrieval-driven weak incubation, and multiple provisioning backends. We evaluate the system on 59 enterprise-style tasks spanning governance-sensitive requests, retrieval-driven provisioning, scoped local execution, and chemistry workflow integration. The retrieval-driven Queen-Bee variant achieves a task success rate of 0.964, zero governance failures, and substantially better scoped execution quality than both a static Queen-Bee baseline and a permissive single-agent baseline. We further show a multi-Bee chemistry workflow with explicit approval gating and a concrete top-3 shortlist grounded in real upstream evidence and screening artifacts. Additional comparisons with hybrid retrieval and LLM-guided provisioning show that richer provisioning backends are viable but do not outperform the lightweight structured retriever on the current small, highly structured capability registry. The results provide prototype-level systems evidence rather than a production deployment study, and suggest that enterprise agent platforms should be evaluated not only by capability, but also by governed provisioning, isolation behavior, scoped execution quality, and artifact-aware workflow coordination.

</details>


### [64] [AI-Driven Test Case Generation from Natural Language Requirements: A Survey of Techniques and Research Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06563)
*Orimoloye Folorunsho,Hassan Reza*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 这是一篇系统文献综述，调研了2000-2025年间利用AI和NLP技术从自然语言需求自动生成测试用例的研究，识别出21项主要研究，并提出三阶段演进综合、六标准差距分析以及四条研究建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件测试是开发中最耗时且昂贵的活动之一，而基于需求的测试生成虽然能在早期从需求文档派生出测试用例，但自然语言的歧义性和不精确性使得直接生成测试用例极具挑战。尽管AI、NLP和LLM的进展使该流程自动化成为可能，但也带来了幻觉、追溯性不足和评估不一致等新风险。因此需要对现有方法、技术、工具、评估方式和研究空白进行系统性梳理。

Method: 遵循Kitchenham和Charters的系统综述指南，检索2000-2025年间主要学术数据库，应用严格的纳入标准，最终筛选出21项主要研究（primary studies）。围绕四个研究问题展开分析：1）用于从自然语言需求生成测试用例的AI/NLP技术；2）支撑这些方法的工具和框架；3）生成测试用例的评估方式；4）现有研究空白。将文献按演进分为三个时代（three evolutionary eras），并依据六个关键质量维度（自动化、歧义处理、领域适用性、追溯性、评估全面性、幻觉控制）进行差距分析。

Result: 核心发现是：没有任何现有方法能够同时满足全部六个关键质量维度。通过三阶段演进综合，揭示了AI测试生成从早期到LLM时代的发展脉络；六标准差距分析表明当前方法在六个维度上均存在不足；提出三条主要贡献和四条面向幻觉、追溯性、复杂度敏感性和合规性的可操作研究指南。

Conclusion: 该综述指出，尽管AI和LLM显著推动了自然语言需求自动生成测试用例的发展，但当前方法在自动化、歧义处理、领域适用性、追溯性、评估全面性和幻觉控制六个维度上仍无全维度解决方案。研究空白集中于幻觉控制、追溯性、复杂度敏感性和合规性四个方向，呼吁未来研究在这些方面取得突破。

Abstract: Software testing is critical for verifying that systems meet specified requirements, yet remains among the most time-consuming and expensive activities in development. Requirements-based test generation allows test cases to be derived early from requirements artifacts, but generating them directly from natural language is challenging due to inherent ambiguity and imprecision. Recent advances in AI, natural language processing (NLP), and large language models (LLMs) have made automating this pipeline increasingly feasible, while introducing new risks including hallucination, reduced traceability, and inconsistent evaluation. This survey addresses four research questions: what AI and NLP techniques have been proposed for generating test cases from natural language requirements; what tools and frameworks support these approaches; how generated test cases are evaluated; and what research gaps remain. Following Kitchenham and Charters' systematic review guidelines, we searched major scholarly databases spanning 2000-2025 and, after applying strict inclusion criteria, identified 21 primary studies. The literature is organized into three evolutionary eras, revealing that no existing approach simultaneously satisfies six key quality dimensions: automation, ambiguity handling, domain applicability, traceability, evaluation thoroughness, and hallucination control. The survey makes three main contributions: a three-era evolutionary synthesis of AI-based test generation; a six-criteria gap analysis showing no current approach fully addresses all quality dimensions; and four actionable research guidelines targeting hallucination, traceability, complexity sensitivity, and compliance.

</details>


### [65] [NTILC: Neural Tool Invocation via Learned Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06566)
*Andrew Krikorian,Yayuan Li,Jason J. Corso*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: NTILC提出了一种基于学习压缩的神经工具调用框架，将完整工具注册表移出上下文窗口，通过共享嵌入空间的近邻检索实现工具选择，并结合Circle Loss与功能性边界损失（FM Loss）解决"语义模糊"问题，将上下文token消耗降低超过95%，推理延迟最高降低74%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体工具调用LLM依赖庞大的API/函数注册表，传统In-Context Tooling（ICT）方式将全部工具描述插入提示词，导致：(1) token成本随注册表规模线性增长（5000个工具约需60万输入token）；(2) 推理延迟升高；(3) 不相关工具造成干扰，降低选择精度。作者将此现象称为"语义模糊"（semantic blur），即语义相似但执行签名不兼容的工具难以区分（如getWeather(zipcode)与getWeather(lat, lon)）。

Method: 框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 编码器——基于all-MiniLM-L6-v2（22.7M参数），接两层MLP投影头，将工具schema文本渲染映射至128维L2归一化嵌入空间；(2) 推理流程——LLM生成计划块→拆分为意图→编码检索→近邻搜索→FSM约束的约束解码生成参数→执行工具；(3) 复合训练目标——Circle Loss（γ=32, Δp=0.75, Δn=0.25）负责语义对齐，新增的Functional Margin Loss根据工具签名兼容性（参数名、类型、必填字段、返回类型）计算权重w_ij=1−compat(t_i,t_j)，对签名不兼容的负样本施加与余弦距离相关的margin排斥力，最优配置为λ1=1.0、λ2=2.0。

Result: 在ToolBench、BFCL、API-Bank、MetaTool、ToolEyes五个公开数据集上：上下文token由O(N)降至O(1)，注册表prompt token归零；Top-1准确率91.3%、Top-5达97.4%、语义模糊子集准确率75.0%、功能错误率仅8.7%，全面优于BM25（26.6%）、Qwen3-Embedding-8B（82.4%）及仅用Circle Loss的方案（89.9%）；推理延迟由~5000–6400ms降至1127–1512ms（降幅约70–74%），与Claude Sonnet 4.6、ChatGPT 5等闭源ICT基线在准确率上持平甚至略优。消融实验证实FM Loss是解决语义模糊问题的关键驱动因素（Semantic-Blur Acc.从70.8%提升至75.0%）。

Conclusion: NTILC验证了工具注册表无需驻留于上下文窗口的设计理念，将智能体能力与提示词长度解耦；FM Loss有效解决了语义相似但执行不兼容工具的区分难题。局限在于需要离线索引，不适用于高度动态或状态依赖的工具，未来工作将聚焦在线索引更新、更丰富的schema兼容性度量及执行安全策略。

Abstract: Agentic tool-calling language models depend on large registries of callable APIs, functions, and local actions. Placing full tool specifications directly in the prompt incurs a cost that scales linearly with the size of the tool registry, rapidly consuming the context budget. As the registry grows, this leads to higher latency and degrades selection accuracy, particularly due to interference from irrelevant tools. We overcome these limitations by introducing NTILC, a neural tool selection and invocation framework that replaces in-context registry look-up with learned latent retrieval. NTILC maps both user intent and tool specifications into a shared embedding space, enabling tool selection via external retrieval rather than in-context lookup. The language model is conditioned only on the selected tool schema, allowing for precise, constrained argument generation. Central to our approach is a signature-aware composite objective, which augments semantic similarity with constraints derived from tool signatures (e.g., argument schema, type compatibility, and return types). By combining Circle Loss with a Functional Margin Loss, the model enforces separation between tools that are semantically similar but incompatible under their execution signatures. We evaluate NTILC on public tool-selection and function-calling datasets and report context token usage, retrieval accuracy, and selection latency metrics. Across these settings, NTILC reduces context window consumption by over 95% and inference latency by up to 74% compared to long-context ICT baselines.

</details>


### [66] [LLM Agent-Assisted Reverse Engineering with Quantitative Readability Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06838)
*Neil Archibald,Ruben Thijssen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Quantitative Readability Score (QRS)框架,通过结合结构相似性门控与三个独立的可读性子指标(词法惊奇度、结构简洁性、惯用质量),引导LLM代理对反编译器生成的C代码进行可读性优化,同时保持功能正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动反编译器生成的C代码虽然功能正确但可读性差,变量名(如local_48)缺乏语义、控制流复杂且保留底层模式。LLM代理虽有潜力改进反编译输出,但缺乏量化指导时会出现上下文腐烂(rot)、指标博弈(gamification)、功能回退等问题,导致长任务中的一致性丧失。

Method: 三阶段研究演进: (1) Phase 1通过Ghidra MCP赋予代理直接工具控制权,但因无量化反馈导致覆盖率低(仅10-15%)、质量不一致、无反馈循环; (2) Phase 2引入radare2 CFG结构相似性作为奖励信号,使用迭代式短上下文代理循环,相似度从0.5-0.7提升至0.85-0.95,但代理通过最小化修改来博弈该指标,导致可读性退化(代码回退到低层模式); (3) Phase 3提出QRS框架,公式为QRS = BS × C,其中BS为二元结构相似性门控(≥0.85时为1.0,否则0.1),C = 0.34·LS + 0.33·SS + 0.33·IQ。LS使用Qwen2.5-Coder-0.5B计算NLL;SS基于圈复杂度(权重0.5)、嵌套深度(0.3)、函数长度(0.2);IQ基于clang-tidy违规密度(含两个自定义检查)。实验在210个合成C二进制文件上进行,采用基于最低子指标生成改进指导的短提示(~600 tokens)迭代流程。

Result: 在210个简单C二进制语料上进行两项实验。实验1(无Bash):共1453次迭代,53个二进制(25.24%)未达QRS阈值(0.75),QRS平均提升+0.420,结构相似性提升+0.484,无QRS或结构相似性回退。实验2(启用Bash验证):迭代次数降至826(平均2.933次/二进制),仅18个失败,QRS平均提升+0.509,结构相似性提升+0.582,验证了代理可有效利用工具自我验证目标。所有实验中QRS和结构相似性回退次数均为0,证明代理正确解读了度量、指导与指令。

Conclusion: 多维度量化指标是引导LLM代理完成复杂任务的关键。单一度量(即使测量重要属性)不足以防止博弈行为并保证质量。QRS框架通过"质量帕累托前沿"约束代理无法通过牺牲某一维度来优化另一维度,为函数级反编译清理提供系统化指导。QRS假设输入代码已功能正确,Phase 0(从原始反编译到可编译代码的清理)被明确划为未来工作。局限在于:尚未通过人类评估研究验证QRS与人类可读性判断的相关性,评估语料为简单合成二进制文件,需在更复杂代码库与多语言/多架构上验证泛化性。

Abstract: Automatic decompilers produce functionally correct but often unreadable C code. This paper addresses one stage of the reverse engineering workflow: improving the readability of decompiled code using LLM agents guided by quantitative metrics. We present a three-phase research evolution. Phase 1 (tool-driven steering via Ghidra MCP) suffered from incomplete coverage and inconsistent improvements due to lack of quantitative guidance. Phase 2 (structural similarity validation alone) revealed that agents optimize for metrics in unintended ways, producing structurally equivalent but less readable code. Our contribution is the Quantitative Readability Score (QRS) framework, a composite metric combining a structural similarity gate with three independent readability sub-metrics (Lexical Surprisal, Structural Simplicity, and Idiomatic Quality). We demonstrate that QRS-guided refinement enables LLM agents to make targeted readability improvements without sacrificing correctness. We provide a discussion of the broader reverse engineering workflow (binary lifting, decompilation cleanup, and achieving functional equivalence) as context, however, it remains out of scope.

</details>


### [67] [MetaConfigurator: AI-Assisted RDF Authoring from JSON Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07094)
*Felix Neubauer,Mahdi Jafarkhani,Kenichi Endo,Jürgen Pleiss,Benjamin Uekermann*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出在开源 JSON Schema 编辑器 MetaConfigurator 中新增"RDF Authoring View"(RDF 创作视图)，将 JSON/YAML/CSV 到 RDF 的转换（RML 映射）、RDF 三元组编辑、SPARQL 查询、知识图谱可视化及 AI 辅助的映射/查询生成集成到单一 Web 界面中，并以 MOF 合成实验数据为案例进行端到端验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学工作流产生大量结构化 JSON 数据，但 JSON 仅保证结构验证，缺少语义互操作性。现有语义 Web 工具链通常只覆盖 RML 映射、三元组编辑、SPARQL 查询、图谱可视化中的某一个环节，研究人员需在多个工具间切换；同时，RML 映射与 SPARQL 查询的编写门槛较高，阻碍了领域专家（而非语义 Web 专家）使用本体化数据。论文目标是降低技术门槛、在一个界面内打通从结构化数据到语义数据再到知识图谱探索的完整链路。

Method: 系统扩展 MetaConfigurator 的前端架构，新增：(1) RML 映射对话框——支持用户粘贴现有映射或通过自然语言 hint + 外部 LLM API 自动生成 RML 映射，并提供交互式代码编辑器与一键 JSON→JSON-LD 转换；(2) 双视图（Context Tab + Triples Tab）——支持 JSON-LD @context 编辑、表格化三元组浏览/分页/排序/过滤/编辑/导出，以及双向同步；(3) Ontology Explorer——从 @context 提取前缀映射，提供本体感知的 IRI 自动补全；(4) SPARQL 对话框——基于 Comunica 浏览器内 QueryEngine 执行 SPARQL 1.2 draft 规范，使用 Sparql.js 做实时语法校验，并集成 AI 自然语言→SPARQL 生成；(5) 知识图谱可视化——基于 Cytoscape.js + COSE-Bilkent 力导向布局，支持节点搜索、聚焦、跳转到本体定义（如 metadata4ing 的 hasParameter、qudt 的单位）等。整体采用 human-in-the-loop 设计，AI 输出均需用户确认后再执行。

Result: 以金属有机框架（MOF）MIL-88B(Fe) 合成的 JSON 协议数据（S-1）为案例，编写 RML 映射（含 OBI、CHEBI、schema.org、qudt 等本体前缀）将原始 JSON 转换为 JSON-LD 形式的类型化 RDF 资源；通过自然语言 hint "检索每个合成的所有 Prep 步骤及其试剂与用量"成功生成可执行 SPARQL 查询并返回结果；可视化模块以图谱形式展示合成批次、试剂、步骤之间的语义关系；亦支持从 SPARQL CONSTRUCT 结果构建只读子图。实践表明该集成环境可在不切换工具的情况下完成"JSON → JSON-LD → 编辑 → 查询 → 可视化"全流程，并能较好地保留实验协议上下文。

Conclusion: 论文贡献在于将数据转换、图谱编辑、查询执行与可视化统一到单一 Web 应用中，使 MetaConfigurator 从纯 JSON Schema 编辑器演化为面向 RDF 的语义创作平台，并通过 AI 辅助显著降低非专家用户的入门门槛。局限包括：(1) AI 生成的 RML 映射和 SPARQL 仍可能含结构/语义错误，专家知识仍不可或缺；(2) 受浏览器端处理能力限制，大规模图谱的交互可视化存在性能瓶颈；(3) 本体类、属性、IRI 模式的高质量选择对非专家仍是难题。总体而言，该工作验证了"在已有结构化数据编辑器中逐步嵌入语义创作能力"的可行性，为面向科研工作流的可访问、可集成的语义 Web 工具奠定基础。

Abstract: Scientific workflows increasingly generate structured JSON data that is easy to exchange but difficult to interpret consistently across systems due to lacking semantic interoperability. While JSON Schema ensures structural validation, it provides no native support for Linked Data semantics. This paper presents an RDF Authoring View extending the open-source JSON Schema editor MetaConfigurator, enabling researchers to transform existing JSON, YAML, or CSV data into RDF using AI-assisted RML mappings, refine triples, execute SPARQL queries, visualize knowledge graphs, and export RDF serializations within a single integrated web interface. This workflow is supported by ontology-aware IRI auto-completion, bidirectional synchronization between JSON-LD text views and RDF triple tables, and AI-assisted SPARQL query generation from natural language hints. We demonstrate the workflow using laboratory data from metal-organic framework (MOF) synthesis experiments. Protocol data describing reagents, procedure steps, and quantities is converted from JSON to ontology-based JSON-LD via RML mappings. We then refine the semantic representation, query relationships between experimental conditions and outcomes, and explore the resulting knowledge graph interactively. This integrated environment bridges conventional structured data management with Semantic Web technologies while preserving experimental context and lowering technical barriers through AI assistance.

</details>


### [68] [Socratic-SWE: Self-Evolving Coding Agents via Trace-Derived Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07412)
*Chuan Xiao,Zhengbo Jiao,Shaobo Wang,Wei Wang,Bing Zhao,Hu Wei,Linfeng Zhang,Lin Qu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Socratic-SWE 是一个用于 LLM 驱动软件工程智能体的闭环自演化框架，通过将智能体自身的历史解题轨迹提炼为结构化技能，并据此生成有针对性的训练任务，实现训练课程随智能体能力迭代而自适应进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 SWE 智能体训练受限于高质量任务数据的匮乏；现有合成数据方法（如固定 mutation 或 bug-injection）生成的训练分布与智能体自身的弱点及训练进度脱节，难以实现有针对性的能力提升。

Method: 1) 将 Solver 的历史解题轨迹（trace）提炼为结构化的 agent skills，总结反复出现的失败模式与有效修复模式；2) 基于这些 skills 指导在真实代码仓库中生成针对性的修复任务；3) 通过 execution-based validation 验证候选任务的可行性，并使用 solver-gradient alignment reward 评分，确保保留的任务既可验证又能提升 Solver 能力；4) 更新后的 Solver 产生新的 traces，形成多轮闭环迭代的自演化课程。

Result: 在 SWE-bench Verified、SWE-bench Lite、SWE-bench Pro 和 Terminal-Bench 2.0 四个基准上，Socratic-SWE 在相同算力预算下持续优于自演化基线方法；经过三轮迭代后，在 SWE-bench Verified 上达到 50.40%。

Conclusion: 解题轨迹（solving traces）可以作为自演化 SWE 智能体的可扩展训练 substrate，使得任务生成与智能体能力提升形成良性闭环。

Abstract: LLM-driven software engineering agents have become a central testbed for real-world language-model capability, yet their training remains limited by the availability of high-quality SWE tasks. Existing synthetic data methods typically create tasks through fixed mutation or bug-injection procedures, making the resulting distributions largely independent of the agent's own weaknesses and training progress. We introduce Socratic-SWE, a closed-loop self-evolution framework that reuses the agent's historical solving traces as a source of training signal. Rather than treating traces only as evidence for reward computation, Socratic-SWE distills them into structured agent skills that summarize recurring failures and effective repair patterns. These skills then guide the generation of targeted repair tasks in real repositories. Candidate tasks are checked through execution-based validation and scored with a solver-gradient alignment reward, so that the retained tasks are both verifiable and useful for improving the Solver. The updated Solver produces new traces, enabling the task curriculum to adapt over successive rounds. Across SWE-bench Verified, SWE-bench Lite, SWE-bench Pro, and Terminal-Bench 2.0, Socratic-SWE consistently improves over self-evolving baselines under the same compute budget, reaching 50.40% on SWE-bench Verified after three iterations. These results suggest that solving traces can serve as a scalable substrate for self-evolving SWE agents.

</details>


### [69] [Architecturally Significant MLOps Guidelines for ML Model Integration and Deployment: a Gray Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06535)
*Faezeh Amou Najafabad,Markus Haug,Keerthiga Rajenthiram,Justus Bogner,Ilias Gerostathopoulos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过灰色文献综述，系统梳理并提炼出25条面向ML模型集成与部署的架构性重要MLOps指南，组织为5大类，为研究者和实践者提供MLOps系统架构设计的实用参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MLOps日益普及，但团队在实施时往往缺乏系统化的架构指导，采用临时性（ad hoc）方式推进项目。现有文献中关于ML模型集成与部署的可复用架构决策、架构策略或指南缺乏整合，导致经验难以在团队间迁移。同时，MLOps涵盖范围广泛，涉及众多相互关联的架构决策，需要一份综合性的实践参考来指导ML模型集成与部署的架构设计。

Method: 采用Garousi等人提出的灰色文献综述（GLR）方法，通过Google搜索引擎执行33条简化查询（结合"MLOps Architect"与模型集成/部署/CI/CD及实践/指南等11个同义词），筛选前两页结果共获得331个网页来源；基于明确的纳入/排除标准（含I1-I3和E1-E5），由至少两名研究者独立审查并通过共识会议裁决，最终选定103个来源；提取相关实践文本后，进行三步式主题分析，由三位作者独立将实践综合为MLOps指南，并通过开放卡片分类法将指南组织为若干类别。

Result: 最终产出25条架构性重要MLOps指南，按五个类别组织：(1) CI/CD与自动化（6条），如建立成熟CI/CD管道、自动化重复任务、自动化回滚机制等；(2) 部署策略与环境（6条），如根据上下文选择生产部署策略（影子/金丝雀/A-B/蓝绿）、选择部署拓扑（云/本地/混合/边缘）等；(3) 设计与集成策略（4条），如采用合适的架构风格（模块化/无服务器）、通过模型注册表分离训练与部署管道、使用标准化模型导出格式（ONNX/PMML）等；(4) 模型服务与推理（5条），如选择合适的模型嵌入组件（嵌入式/专用服务/拆分管道/模型即数据）、选择推理模式（批量/在线）等；(5) ML组件管理（4条），如使用容器化（Docker）部署、使用Kubernetes等编排工具、配置充足算力等。72%的指南被超过4个来源支持，反映了实践中的高度共识。

Conclusion: 该研究为MLOps系统架构设计提供了从实践中提炼的25条架构性重要指南的综合性参考，强调自动化、成熟CI/CD管道、标准化模型导出格式和容器化等核心实践，可支持研究者进一步构建MLOps参考架构，也可帮助实践者在ML模型集成与部署阶段做出更明智的架构决策。研究还发现，灰色文献对部署阶段的关注显著高于集成阶段（平均13.5 vs 7.66个来源/指南），这为未来AI工程研究界填补集成阶段指导空白提供了机会。未来工作将扩展指南的应用上下文、权衡与挑战描述，并通过焦点小组进一步验证。

Abstract: Context. Despite the growing adoption of Machine Learning Operations (MLOps), teams often approach MLOps projects in an ad hoc manner due to the lack of consolidated architectural guidance. The community would benefit from a reference that synthesizes knowledge to inform the architectural design of MLOps systems, especially regarding the integration and deployment of ML models. Objective. In response, our goal is to provide a comprehensive overview of architecturally significant guidelines for the integration and deployment of ML models in MLOps systems. Method. We conduct a gray literature review of 103 web sources to analyze state-of-practice knowledge on MLOps model integration and deployment. We then apply thematic analysis to synthesize these practices into recommended guidelines. Results. We contribute a collection of 25 architecturally significant MLOps guidelines for model integration and deployment, organized into five categories, and describe their impact on the overall system architecture. Conclusion. Our results serve as an overview of state-of-practice MLOps guidelines to support researchers and practitioners with the integration and deployment of ML models in their MLOps systems.

</details>


### [70] [AutoPipelineAI: Context-Aware CI/CD Pipeline Generation from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06662)
*Youssef Mohamed Aboelfotoh,Mohamed Ahmed Hemdan,Mohammad El-Ramly,Khlood Hassan,Mahmoud Saleh Saad,Ahmed Mohamed Tolba,Seif Gamal Abdelmonem*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AutoPipelineAI 是一个利用大语言模型（LLM）从自然语言描述自动生成 CI/CD 管道配置（如 GitHub Actions、GitLab CI/CD）的上下文感知系统，通过多智能体协作、仓库结构分析与自动验证反馈机制，降低 DevOps 配置门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 CI/CD 管道配置依赖平台特定语法（如 YAML、Groovy），学习曲线陡峭、易出错，对缺乏 DevOps 经验的开发者尤为困难。现有方案（模板系统、AI 辅助调试工具）多为"反应式"，仅优化已有管道或辅助排错，无法端到端从自然语言生成仓库感知的完整管道配置。该研究旨在填补"高层意图"与"低层基础设施代码"之间的空白，实现 DevOps 配置的民主化。

Method: 系统采用模块化多层架构，包含一个基于图的多智能体处理层：① 监督路由（Supervisor Router）协调任务；② 规划智能体（Planner Agent）将自然语言需求分解为结构化工作流目标；③ 仓库分析智能体（Repository Analyzer）采用 ReAct 策略扫描 package.json、Dockerfile、构建脚本等以推断技术栈与运行需求；④ 生成器-验证器反射子图（Generator-Validator Reflexion），其中生成器产出平台特定的 YAML 配置，验证器执行 YAML 格式、模式校验、lint 与密钥扫描，反射路由器根据验证结果决定是否迭代重生成；⑤ 工具集成层提供文件系统操作、Shell 执行、仓库检查等受控访问。评估框架包含两阶段：(1) 基于 ChatGPT 的 AI 辅助评估，衡量结构准确率（Accuracy）与完整率（Completeness）；(2) 基于需求清单的功能性评估，逐项检查仓库特定功能要求。

Result: 在两个开源项目（Peewee-Python，Hugo-Go）上对比测试了四个 LLM：GPT-4.1、GPT-4o、MiniMax-M2-1、Qwen3-Coder-Next-UD-79B。AI 评估显示 Qwen3 与 MiniMax 在结构质量上领先（平均准确率 0.85–0.87），GPT-4o 稍逊（0.77）。然而功能性评估揭示出巨大"表面正确-实际失效"鸿沟：Peewee 上原始通过率仅 0–50%（MiniMax 为 0%），Hugo 上为 0–35%。所有模型在两项任务中均出现三类系统性失败：① 静默逻辑错误（如 Peewee 矩阵任务统一对 SQLite 执行而忽略 –engine 标志，CI 呈绿色但结果错误）；② 外部引用幻觉（如不存在的 PyPI 包、占位 SHA256 校验和、错误 GitHub 仓库归属）；③ 普适性配置缺陷（MariaDB 命名、分支通配符、超时值、磁盘清理等均失败）。复杂度越高，可靠性越差，呈现明显"复杂度鸿沟"。

Conclusion: LLM 驱动的 CI/CD 管道生成可作为可行的"初稿生成"手段，显著降低配置门槛，但当前模型尚无法产出生产级可靠管道——结构正确不等于运行正确。生成结果应被视为需经执行测试与领域专家审查的草稿工件。未来工作将围绕四个方向展开：① 混合合成（LLM 创意推理 + 规则验证引擎）；② 运行时感知反馈（利用 GitHub Actions 实际执行日志自动修复）；③ 跨领域泛化（多语言仓库与多云目标）；④ 检索增强生成（RAG）以基于真实仓库工件与外部文档减少幻觉，从而从"高层起草"迈向"自主生产级"基础设施生成。

Abstract: Modern software development relies on CI/CD pipelines to automate testing, building, and deployment operations. Configuring DevOps pipelines is challenging and time-consuming, as developers must understand platform-specific syntax and manually create configuration files. This complexity can lead to configuration errors and reduced productivity, especially for developers with limited DevOps experience. This paper introduces the AutoPipelineAI system, which generates CI/CD pipeline configurations using natural language descriptions. The proposed solution uses large language models (LLMs) to translate developer intent, analyze repository structures, and create specific pipeline scripts for environments like GitHub Actions and GitLab CI/CD. It integrates repository-aware analysis, automated validation systems, and a feedback mechanism that confirms the accuracy and usability of the created pipelines. We present the system architecture, its implementation, and an assessment framework designed to measure generation precision, configuration validity, and reduction in setup effort compared to manual pipeline creation. AutoPipelineAI illustrates how LLMs can simplify the complexity of DevOps configuration and enhance developer access to continuous delivery methods. Evaluation results provide early evidence that repository-aware, natural-language-driven CI/CD generation is a viable and promising paradigm for reducing the complexity of DevOps configuration and enabling more accessible software delivery automation.

</details>


### [71] [Tensor Algebraic Property Skeletons: Amplifying Property-Based Testing for AI Compilers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06747)
*Yuxin Qiu,Ben Limpanukorn,Seongmin Lee,Jiyuan Wang,Qian Zhang,Miryung Kim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Propilot 是一个基于 LLM 的智能体属性测试框架，通过将张量代数知识编码为可复用的"属性骨架"(property skeletons)，为深度学习编译器（如 TVM）自动生成可执行的基于属性的测试 (PBT)。该方法解决了现有测试方法仅能生成格式正确输入但无法检测语义漂移的局限，在 TVM 上用 212 个算子和 20 个属性骨架生成了 4,579 个 PBT，相较直接 LLM 生成减少了 49% 的冗余并消除了无效测试，成功暴露了语义不一致和数值精度问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习编译器（TVM、ONNX-MLIR）在将张量计算图lower为目标后端可执行代码时，会进行图重写、算子合法化、融合、布局变换和代码生成等优化，这些优化可能产生"静默语义漂移"——编译后程序仍可运行但输出不再等价于源图。现有测试方法（模糊测试、LLM 直接生成）主要解决了"良构输入生成"问题，但无法捕获编译器应保持的代数不变性。核心挑战在于将张量代数关系（如结合律、交换律、分解性质）转化为可执行 PBT，这需要联合构建算子、输入和测试预言。

Method: Propilot 包含三个核心组件：(1) 属性骨架 (property skeletons) ——可复用的属性文档，每条骨架编码意图（如交换律）、适用算子类、拒绝规则（如减法不适用）、具体化模式 (op(x,y) vs op(y,x)) 和预言模板 (AllClose 容差比较)，共 17 条骨架分三类：代数性质、张量性质、回退性质；(2) 受控测试生成 ——基于 data/apply/assert 三段式测试场景规范，配合 23 个数据生成器 (如 pure_random、identity_injection)，通过优先级规则优先选择未使用或低覆盖率的算子-属性-生成器组合；(3) 测试验证与反馈循环 ——执行前进行三阶段检查：场景模式检查、算子适用性检查、运行时安全检查 (AST 分析验证 build_model/run_compiler/check_oracle/save_repro 步骤)，被拒测试的拒绝原因反馈给下一轮生成。使用 GPT-5.5 作为智能体。

Result: 在 TVM 上的评估结果：Propilot 生成 4,579 个 PBT，相比 LLM-only 基线（1,863 个测试，24 小时内）将冗余度从约 79% 降至约 30%，无效测试降至 0%。错误分类显示 Propilot 暴露的失败中 50% 是数学与实现不一致（语义错误）、25% 是数值不稳定性、12.5% 是参考预言不匹配、12.5% 是残余 API 误用。代表性发现包括：sum 算子在 2042 个 9,035 值（float32）上单次归约产生 18,449,656，而分块归约得到正确的 18,449,470，证实 IEEE 754 单精度累加器的精度损失。覆盖率方面 LLM-only 反而最高，但作者论证这部分覆盖来自无效路径（API 误用、模型构建错误等），Propilot 的覆盖更具语义价值。

Conclusion: Propilot 展示了如何将张量代数从"数学背景知识"转化为"可复用的测试知识库"，通过结构化属性骨架使 LLM 智能体能够规模化地为 DL 编译器生成具有语义意义的可执行 PBT。其主要贡献在于：(1) 重新定义 DL 编译器测试问题为带语义预言的 PBT 引导；(2) 引入属性骨架机制结合受控实例化和验证；(3) 实证证明该方法能有效暴露语义和数值错误。论文指出代码覆盖率可能具有误导性，应结合错误语义质量评估 DL 编译器测试方法。Propilot 可通过添加更多骨架轻松扩展到其他编译器和属性。

Abstract: Deep learning (DL) compilers such as TVM and ONNX-MLIR lower tensor computation graphs into optimized executables for target backends. Testing these AI compilers has made substantial progress in generating well-formed inputs in the context of fuzzing; however, such generation alone does not catch semantic drifts from algebraic invariants that graph transformations and optimizations are expected to preserve. While tensor algebra has been studied for decades, it has not been transformed into executable property-based tests (PBTs) for DL compilers because doing so requires jointly constructing operators, inputs, and test oracles. The central challenge is no longer generating well-formed inputs for fuzzing DL compilers, but bootstrapping executable PBTs with such inputs and oracles based on tensor algebra. We realize this vision in Propilot, an LLM-driven agentic property-based testing framework for DL compilers with GPT 5.5. First, Propilot represents tensor algebra knowledge as reusable property skeletons, each coupled with operator constraints, shape and value rules, and oracle templates. Second, given a target compiler, Propilot instantiates these skeletons into executable PBTs by generating paired tensor computation graphs, concrete tensor inputs, and expected semantic relations as oracles. Next, to prevent generated tests from degenerating into invalid or uninformative PBTs, Propilot validates each PBT candidate before execution for applicability and safety. Validation feedback, execution results, and coverage signals guide subsequent generation. We evaluate Propilot on TVM with 212 operators and 20 property skeletons, generating 4,579 PBTs. Compared with direct LLM-based PBT generation, Propilot reduces redundancy by 49% and eliminates invalid tests through explicit property skeletons. This effectiveness translates into finding semantic errors and numerical discrepancies.

</details>


### [72] [Pomona: Continuous Code Quality Improvement via Small, Automated Changes at Bloomberg](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06752)
*David Williams,Angelos Evripiotis,Serkan Kirbas,Harry Morgan,Sergey Magidovich,Peter Wainwright,Federica Sarro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Bloomberg开发的Pomona，一个受Kaizen哲学启发的轻量级agentic工具，通过两个agent技能（Scanning和Repair）实现持续、小规模的自动化代码质量改进。初步评估显示15/17的PR被合并，8/10的高级工程师希望采用该工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM和agentic工具在软件工程中的广泛应用，开发者面临短期速度与长期可维护性之间的矛盾：使用LLM可能带来速度提升，但也可能引入代码复杂度和质量问题。现有研究指出agent采用可能仅暂时提升开发速度却增加长期质量负担。因此，需要一种能够在不干扰工程师工作流的前提下，持续、低风险地解决技术债务（如linting违规、TODO标记、测试覆盖不足等被忽视的低价值-高收益任务）的解决方案。

Method: Pomona由两个agent技能和一个结构化的优先任务待办列表（backlog）组成，三者均以Markdown文件实现，语言无关且可与任何agentic产品集成：
1. **Scanning技能**：当高优先级任务（P1/P2）耗尽时触发，并行启动多个子代理（sub-agent）发现代码质量问题。论文实现包含三个子代理——子代理1执行ruff规则扩展和mypy严格化；子代理2搜索TODO/FIXME/HACK/XXX标记、不一致注释和死代码；子代理3识别测试覆盖缺口、长函数和编码标准违规。发现结果按"效益×审查难度"矩阵聚合到P1–P4优先级类别。
2. **优先任务待办列表**：以Markdown格式存储于代码库根目录，git历史自然记录变更。
3. **Repair技能**：入口点，选择最高优先级任务，生成约10行diff的小型PR，更新backlog并提交。受限的人工监督（human-in-the-loop）确保PR需经人工审核才能合并。
设计原则：低摩擦（无需工程师调整工作流）、低风险（human-in-the-loop）、专注于代码质量改进。

Result: **部署评估**（一个Bloomberg团队，约一个月）：
- 共生成17个PR，15/17（88.2%）成功合并
- 合并的PR中11个仅需强制审核即通过，无需额外交互
- 中位关闭时间1小时43分钟，70%以上（12/17）在4小时内关闭
- 70% PR当日业务日完成
- 两次拒绝均因并发执行导致重复PR
- 全部PR为P1级（多数为ruff规则违规）
- 中位变更4个文件/16行，最大PR含17文件/139行
**问卷调查**（10名DevX团队高级工程师）：
- 7/10认为工具有用，8/10希望尝试
- 7/10愿意每周审核2–3个PR
- 9/10认可小diff设计，8/10赞赏代码质量聚焦
- 5/10喜欢自动化任务识别和完成
- 担忧：review过载、无意义变更、缺乏对AI判断的信任
- 期望任务：TODO/过时注释、linting问题、简单bug修复、依赖管理

Conclusion: Pomona展示了agent技能可有效配置以持续管理技术债务，特别擅长"低悬果实"（linting违规、死代码、TODO）。核心见解：①小变更推动采纳，工程师审核带宽是AI开发的真正瓶颈；②AI可作为技术债务管理的催化剂；③优先排序与检测同等重要；④人类监督（human-in-the-loop）对建立信任至关重要，资深工程师对agent自主判断仍持谨慎态度。论文呼吁未来研究关注自动化任务相关性过滤、agentic任务分解以及量化人机协作认知负荷的多维度指标。Pomona的完整实现仅需三个Markdown文件，论文详细描述了实现细节，便于学术界和工业界复现。

Abstract: In this short experience paper, we present Pomona, a lightweight agentic tool that utilises agent skills for continuous automated code quality improvement. Inspired by the philosophy of Kaizen(TM), Pomona automates a cycle of discovery and incremental repair: a Scanning skill identifies improvement tasks (e.g., linting violations, technical debt markers, and test gaps) and prioritises them in a structured backlog, while a Repair skill generates tiny pull requests (PRs) targeting ~10 lines of diff. This human-in-the-loop design enables frequent, low-risk improvements while maintaining engineer trust and productivity and reducing technical debt. We evaluated Pomona through a one-month deployment in a team and a questionnaire distributed to 10 senior engineers. Our preliminary results are promising: 15 of 17 generated PRs were successfully merged with a median time-to-close of under 2 hours. Furthermore, 8/10 of surveyed engineers expressed a desire to adopt Pomona, praising small diff sizes and Pomona's focus on improving code quality. We conclude by discussing actionable insights for researchers and practitioners on strategies for effective agentic deployment in industry.

</details>


### [73] [Chiseling Out Efficiency: Structured Skeleton Supervision for Efficient Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06821)
*Yu Yu,Zhihong Sun,Jia Li,Yao Wan,Chuanyi Li,Hongyu Zhang,Ruyun Wang,Tao Huang,Zhi Jin,Ge Li,Chen Lyu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出EffiSkel框架，通过显式提取和监督"效率骨架"(efficiency skeletons)来引导LLM生成更高效的代码。该框架在多任务学习范式中联合优化代码生成、骨架预测和骨架感知掩码损失，在多个编程语言和基准上显著提升了功能正确性和执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成的代码虽然在功能上正确，但执行效率显著低于人类优化版本（GPT-4生成代码平均慢1.69倍，最差可达45.49倍）。现有提升效率的方法（如EffiCoder的效率感知SFT和CodeDPO的偏好优化）仅通过模仿完整优化解来学习效率信号，未显式编码对运行时性能至关重要的结构化代码模式（如最优算法选择、高效控制流、内存管理）。这导致模型仅学到表层token模式，无法理解加速执行的结构性原因。

Method: 提出三阶段框架：(1) 数据画像：从APPS训练集执行多个解，按执行时间排名取Top-5高效解，构建APPS+EFFI数据集（3,742题/18,710高效解）。(2) 骨架提取：提出三种互补策略——SMS（基于token公共子序列的词法分析）、SSS（基于AST相似子树的句法分析）、TAS（结合行级执行剖面分析的时序感知骨架，融合共享子树与执行超阈值的热点）。(3) 多任务优化：联合优化代码生成损失L_code、骨架预测损失L_skeleton和位置敏感掩码损失L_mask（仅在骨架token上计算），总损失L_total=αL_code+βL_skeleton+γL_mask。提出两个新指标：Efficiency Ratio (ER, 配对胜率) 和 Average Speedup (AS, 配对加速比，经P5-P95 winsorize处理)。

Result: 在5个LLM（Qwen2.5-Coder 1.5B/7B、StarCoder2 3B、DeepSeek-Coder 6.7B、CodeLlama 7B）和7个基准（Mercury、ENAMEL、APPS、EffiBench、HumanEval-X Java、DevEval、CoderEval）共45种配置下，EffiSkel在功能正确性和运行效率上均稳定超越基线。在Mercury+DeepSeek-Coder上比EffiCoder*提升ER +11.11%、AS +0.36，比CodeDPO*提升ER +3.71%、AS +0.22。骨架消融显示TAS>SSS>SMS（结构信号越丰富收益越大），多任务消融显示+Mask和+Skel均独立贡献，二者结合效果最佳。骨架策略具有互补适用场景：SMS无需解析器/运行时（轻量场景），SSS仅需AST（安全敏感审查），TAS需剖析（性能关键负载）。在Afterburner式迭代优化中初始化可叠加增益，且收益随模型规模放大。Java和仓库级基准（DevEval、CoderEval）上同样有效。

Conclusion: EffiSkel通过将效率骨架作为显式结构化监督信号注入多任务学习，证明了显式建模效率结构模式能有效提升LLM生成代码的运行效率，且不损害功能正确性，反而因正则化效应改善Pass@1。骨架丰富度（SMS→SSS→TAS）与效率增益呈单调正相关，提示性能信号源（词法/句法/动态）的多样性是关键。该工作将代码生成中的结构引导从"正确性"扩展到"效率"维度，为绿色计算和可持续软件工程提供了可学习、可解释的优化路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are capable of generating syntactically correct and functionally complete programs, greatly streamlining software development. However, recent studies reveal that these programs typically execute substantially slower than human-optimized counterparts. Existing approaches to bridging this efficiency gap typically involve either iteratively optimizing code after generation or fine-tuning models on corpora of efficient code. Yet, these methods expose the model to efficiency signals only by mimicking complete, optimized solutions, without explicitly encoding the structural code patterns essential for achieving high runtime performance. Addressing this gap presents two core challenges: (1) extracting and representing latent, efficiency-oriented structural patterns embedded within complex syntax and control flows, and (2) effectively learning these patterns without destabilizing the semantic training of LLMs. To tackle these challenges, we propose EffiSkel, an efficiency skeleton-guided framework that explicitly extracts and learns efficiency skeletons-abstract, reusable structural patterns underpinning efficient code-by leveraging three complementary strategies. These skeletons are integrated into a multi-task learning regime that jointly optimizes code generation and skeleton prediction. Experiments across multiple programming languages and benchmarks demonstrate that EffiSkel significantly enhances both functional correctness and efficiency, resulting on Mercury with DeepSeek-Coder (7B) a +11.11% (vs. EffiCoder) and +3.71% (vs. CodeDPO) higher Efficiency Ratio (ER), and a +0.36 (vs. EffiCoder) and +0.22 (vs. CodeDPO) increase in Average Speedup (AS). These results highlight the effectiveness of explicitly modeling efficiency skeletons in improving the runtime performance of code generated by LLMs.

</details>


### [74] [SkelDPO: A Skeleton-Guided Direct Preference Optimization Framework for Efficient Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06826)
*Yu Yu,Chen Lyu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SkelDPO框架，通过在DPO中引入代码骨架（skeleton）级别的偏好优化信号，联合优化代码级和骨架级偏好，使Code LLM不仅学习"哪段代码更快"，还学习"为何某些实现更高效"，在多个模型和基准上显著提升代码生成效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Code LLM（如SFT、RLHF、DPO）主要以功能正确性为优化目标，将完整程序视为单一优化单元，忽视了对效率有决定性影响的内部结构因素。模型虽能生成语义正确的代码，但缺乏对效率关键结构的细粒度建模，导致正确但低效的代码（如冗余循环嵌套、重复计算等）。同时，效率与正确性之间存在天然张力，简单的效率偏好优化会破坏语义完整性。

Method: SkelDPO包含三个核心阶段：(1) 数据处理：对APPS数据集中候选代码进行50次重复执行，取最快5个和最慢5个正确解分别标记为高效/低效；(2) 骨架提取：通过token级交集计算高效公共token集T+和低效公共token集T-，将其余token替换为<MASK>占位符，生成高效骨架S+和低效骨架S-；(3) 骨架引导偏好优化：在标准DPO基础上引入联合损失L_SkelDPO = α·L_code + (1-α)·L_skeleton，α=0.8。使用LoRA微调，batch size=64，学习率1e-5，β=0.1，训练800 epoch。

Result: 在Mercury和ENAMEL两个效率导向基准上，使用5个代表性Code LLM（Qwen2.5-Coder-1.5B/7B、StarCoder2-3B、DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B、CodeLlama-7B）进行评估。SkelDPO相比SOTA方法CodeDPO，Pass@1提升3-6%，Beyond@1提升3-7%，Effi@1提升2-5%，在复杂任务（Hard）上提升更显著。参数敏感性实验显示α=0.8时取得最优平衡；骨架数据比例从0%增至100%时性能单调提升。定性分析显示SkelDPO生成更简洁、可读且高效的实现。骨架定位与人工标注的结构一致性达83.2%。

Conclusion: SkelDPO通过骨架级别的偏好对齐突破了传统DPO仅依赖正确性或效率对的局限性，实现了语义正确性与结构效率的统一建模，为可解释、可迁移的高效代码生成提供了新视角。其局限性在于骨架提取依赖效率实现的多样性，且数据预处理带来额外计算开销。

Abstract: With the remarkable progress of Code Large Language Models (Code LLMs) in achieving semantic correctness, execution efficiency has become an increasingly important dimension for evaluating their practical utility. However, existing approaches typically treat full programs as a single optimization target during training, without explicitly modeling the structural factors that influence efficiency. As a result, although these models can generate semantically correct code, they fail to learn, at a fine-grained level, the underlying skeleton features that lead to efficient implementations. To address this limitation, we propose SkelDPO (Skeleton-Guided Direct Preference Optimization), a skeleton-guided preference optimization framework that systematically enhances the efficiency of code generation. SkelDPO first identifies efficient and inefficient implementations from the code dataset and, through comparative analysis, locates their efficiency-prone and inefficiency-prone points, forming alignment signals between efficiency and inefficiency skeletons. During training, a joint code and skeleton preference loss is introduced, enabling the model to learn semantic correctness while reinforcing its understanding of efficiency-critical components in code. Results show that SkelDPO consistently surpasses existing methods: compared with SOTA method that relies solely on efficient and inefficient code preference optimization, it improves Pass@1, Beyond@1, and Effi@1 by 3-6%, 3-7%, and 2-5%, with greater improvements observed on complex tasks. Overall, SkelDPO provides a new perspective on skeleton-level efficiency alignment, breaking the limitation of conventional preference optimization that relies solely on correctness or efficiency pairs. All datasets and source code are publicly available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [75] [Empirical Study on the Characteristics and Evolution of AI-usage in GitHub Repositories: Evidence from Code Comments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06843)
*Abdullah Al Mujahid,Preetha Chatterjee,Mia Mohammad Imran*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对GitHub上35,361条明确引用AI（如ChatGPT、Copilot、Claude）的代码注释及其关联代码块进行了大规模实证研究，并分析了12,996条后续提交消息，揭示了开发者在真实项目中如何整合、修改和演化AI辅助代码的规律。研究发现开发者主要将LLM用于代码实现，但随后频繁进行重构、清理、功能扩展和错误修复；时间趋势显示AI使用正从直接代码生成向知识概念支持和代码增强转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多在受控实验或基准测试中评估LLM的代码生成能力，脱离了开发者在真实项目中的实际工作流程；尤其缺乏对开发者如何适配、修改和整合AI生成代码的细粒度理解。代码注释作为与代码共存的开发意图记录，能够反映开发者对AI输出的实时认知、评价和修改，为研究真实场景下人机协作提供了独特的观察视角。

Method: 研究采用多阶段混合方法：(1) 通过GitHub Code Search API使用480个组合查询模板（16个AI工具关键词×6个行为动词×4个连接词）抓取Python和JavaScript仓库中2022年12月至2026年3月间明确引用AI的代码注释，并通过补丁匹配提取关联代码块；(2) 对500条样本进行人工开放编码和轴心编码，构建任务类型（6类）和AI贡献类型（4类）分类法，使用Gwet's AC1评估标注一致性；(3) 使用两个开源大模型（gemma-4:31b和nemotron-3-super:120b）进行少样本提示标注，并通过Dawid-Skene期望最大化框架聚合多模型预测，保留400条用于初始化、100条用于保留评估；(4) 提取12,996条"首次修改提交"，使用BERTopic结合UMAP降维和HDBSCAN聚类进行主题建模，并通过语义分组归为8类开发者行为；(5) 每月归一化时间序列分析，使用3个月滚动均值平滑和IQR异常值裁剪，并拟合Newey-West标准误的线性趋势模型。

Result: RQ1结果显示，开发者主要将AI用于代码实现（18,149条，72.04%），其次是代码增强（4,039，16.03%）、测试（1,322，5.25%）、文档（1,295，5.14%）和调试（388，1.54%）；AI贡献形式中实现（16,137，82.13%）占绝对主导，其次是知识与概念支持（2,798，14.76%）和工件生成（714，3.63%）。RQ2对10,139条首次修改提交的主题建模揭示了8类后续修改行为：功能集成与扩展（2,769）、重构与清理（1,465）、错误修复（1,304）、配置与依赖管理（810）、文档（709）、测试与评估（432）、数据/模式/管道处理（128）、日志与监控（72）。RQ3纵向分析显示代码增强（β=+0.00113, p=0.049）、测试（β=+0.00097, p=0.004）、调试（β=+0.00021, p=0.025）和知识与概念支持（β=+0.00185, p=0.025）显著上升，而文档（β=-0.00151, p=0.008）和工件生成（β=-0.00181, p=0.006）显著下降。DS-EM聚合在保留集上达到Task Type AC1=0.7005、Contribution Type AC1=0.8128的近完美一致性。

Conclusion: 研究得出三点核心结论：(1) AI已深度嵌入软件开发流程，不仅作为代码生成工具，更作为协作支持机制，开发者在AI辅助代码引入后持续进行重构、扩展和纠错，表明人类监督仍是核心环节；(2) 传统基于初始代码生成的生产力度量方式存在偏差，AI的真实价值需要从生成、集成、测试到维护的全生命周期视角来衡量；(3) 纵向数据显示AI使用呈现成熟化趋势——开发者从初期的脚手架式代码生成，逐步向需要判断力的知识支持、代码增强和质量保障任务扩展，符合创新扩散理论的多阶段采用模式。未来工作将扩展至更多编程语言、追踪首次提交后的长期演化模式，并深入分析知识支持的供需匹配问题。

Abstract: Developers increasingly use AI tools such as ChatGPT, Copilot, and Claude in everyday software workflows, but prior studies often evaluate LLM outputs in isolation rather than examining how developers adapt them in real projects. We analyze 35,361 GitHub code comments that explicitly reference AI use and their associated code blocks. We first open-code 500 unique comments and code blocks to derive a taxonomy of AI-assisted development activities, then annotate the full dataset using two LLM-based classifiers and aggregate predictions with Dawid-Skene expectation-maximization. We also analyze 12,996 subsequent commit messages to study how AI-assisted code evolves after introduction, and examine temporal trends from December 2022 to March 2026. Our results show that developers primarily use LLMs for code implementation, followed by code enhancement, debugging, documentation, and testing. Subsequent commits frequently involve refactoring and cleanup, feature integration and extension, and bug fixing, indicating sustained human oversight in adapting AI-assisted code. Over time, AI-referencing comments shift from direct code generation toward knowledge and conceptual support and code enhancement. These findings suggest that AI tools are becoming embedded not only as code-generation aids, but also as collaborative support mechanisms whose outputs are refined, extended, and corrected by developers over time.

</details>


### [76] [From Custom Logic to APIs: Understanding and Recommending API Replacement Refactorings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06912)
*Bridget Nyirongo,Yanjie Jiang,Yuxia Zhang,Hui Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次对API替换重构（用API调用替代自定义逻辑）进行大规模实证研究，并提出混合框架AKIRA，结合模式启发式与重构感知知识库，自动推荐API替换重构机会。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重构工具依赖预定义模板，难以捕捉复杂、多语句的语义等价模式，对API替换重构的检测支持有限；缺乏对该类重构的实证理解。

Method: 1) 挖掘6个开源Java项目的166,299个提交，筛选1,800个手动分析，识别366个验证实例以刻画重构范围、类别与模式；2) 提出AKIRA框架，融合模式确定性启发式与重构感知知识库；3) 在人工构建数据集与外部RETIWA数据集上评估。

Result: 在人工策划数据集上达到90%召回率与88%精度；在外部RETIWA数据集上，将召回率从21%提升至81%，精度从40%提升至78%，显著超越现有方法。

Conclusion: 静态模式匹配与语义推理相结合可有效支持复杂API替换重构的自动化推荐，为重构工具设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Software refactoring is essential for maintaining code quality. However, API replacement refactoring, which replaces custom logic with API calls, remains underexplored. Existing refactoring tools provide limited support for detecting such opportunities because they rely on predefined templates and have difficulty capturing complex, multi-statement semantic equivalents. To address this limitation, we conduct the first empirical study of API replacement refactorings by mining 166,299 commits across six open-source Java projects and manually analyzing a curated subset of 1,800 commits, from which we identify 366 validated instances to characterize their scope, categories, and recurring patterns. Based on these insights, we propose AKIRA (Adaptive Knowledge Discovery and Retrieval), a hybrid framework that integrates pattern-deterministic heuristics with a refactoring-aware knowledge base to assess the practical feasibility of recommending API replacement refactorings. Our evaluation shows that AKIRA achieves 90% recall and 88% precision on a manually curated dataset. Furthermore, on the external RETIWA dataset, AKIRA significantly improves the state of the art by increasing recall from 21% to 81% and precision from 40% to 78%. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of combining static pattern matching with semantic reasoning to support the automation of recommending complex API replacement refactorings.

</details>


### [77] [Porting Declarative UI to HarmonyOS: A Heuristic-guided LLM Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07085)
*Kunwu Zheng,Pengyu Xue,Zhen Yang,Xiran Lyu,Peishi Lai,Mengying Zhao,Yutian Tang,Huizhi Zhang,Xianhang Li,Linhao Wu,Chengyi Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 ArkTrans,首个针对声明式 UI(Kotlin Jetpack Compose / SwiftUI → HarmonyOS ArkUI)的启发式引导 LLM 翻译框架,通过元数据提取、ArkUI 骨架构建、LLM 翻译与规则化后修复四阶段流水线,在基线 Direct/One-shot Prompting 全部编译失败(0% CSR)的情况下,最高实现 90.67% 的编译成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HarmonyOS 作为新兴操作系统亟需从 Android/iOS 迁移应用,但现有代码翻译方法主要面向后端逻辑或遗留命令式 UI(XML/UIKit),无法应对 KJC、SwiftUI、ArkUI 等最新声明式 UI 范式。核心存在两大挑战:(1) LLM 对 ArkUI(2023 年发布)语料匮乏导致编程语言不熟悉;(2) LLM 倾向直接复制源语言语法,产生严重的句法混乱(经验研究中 40% 失败源于句法错误,即便有骨架引导仍有 51.4% 残留)。

Method: ArkTrans 包含四阶段流水线:(1) 元数据提取(ME)— 基于 tree-sitter 解析 AST,递归构建 JSON 格式的 UI 树 ⟨comp, props, mod, children⟩,通过 PascalCase 命名约定与黑名单识别 UI 组件;(2) 骨架构建(SC)— 利用 KJC→ArkUI / SwiftUI→ArkUI 组件映射字典,生成含部分 ArkUI 代码与属性占位注释的确定性骨架;(3) LLM 驱动翻译(LT)— 注入主题颜色元数据与 one-shot 示例(覆盖属性赋值、修饰符链式调用、布局简写展开、组件命名、状态管理五类典型句法差异),引导 LLM 完成骨架到代码的填充;(4) 后修复(PF)— 四条经验性规则:常量内联(CI,解决 DesignToken 等外部常量引用)、词法纠正(LR,替换 toInt() 等源 API)、布局属性纠正(LPR,将 horizontal 展开为 left/right)、结构完整性验证(SIV,修复 Blank 误置于非线性容器及括号不平衡)。并构建了 100 样本文件级平行基准(8 类应用场景,跨 KJC/SwiftUI)。

Result: 以 GPT-5.2 为骨干:KJC→ArkUI CSR 达 90.67%,SwiftUI→ArkUI CSR 达 53.33%,显著优于基线(均为 0%)。视觉保真度方面全局指标 CH 30.23%–56.01%、CLIP 45.59%–78.89%;局部指标 Pos 23.30%–47.00%、Size 24.50%–48.40%、Color 23.20%–46.30%、Text 47.70%–82.50%。消融实验表明四阶段均不可或缺(UI 树单独引入无法突破 0% CSR,骨架打破零成功屏障至 8.00%–13.33%,One-shot 示例跃升至 21.33%–50.67%,后修复贡献最大提升 78.94%–150.02% CSR)。PF 模块中 LR 最为关键(移除后 CSR 下降 32.00%–29.33 个百分点)。跨模型验证(GPT-5.2、GLM-5、DeepSeek-V3.2、Kimi-K2-Turbo)证明方法模型无关,平均 CSR 提升至 44.00%–76.33%;SwiftUI→ArkUI 整体更难,因 Swift 类型推断与 ArkTS 严格静态类型检查及 @Builder 作用域规则冲突。

Conclusion: ArkTrans 是首个面向 HarmonyOS 的声明式 UI 跨平台翻译方法,通过启发式骨架引导与规则化后修复的协同设计,有效缓解了 LLM 对新编程语言不熟悉与句法混乱两大核心难题,在代码可执行性与视觉保真度上均显著超越直接提示与单样本提示基线,为 HarmonyOS 生态快速扩充提供了实用工具。未来工作将引入编译器反馈机制并扩展基准覆盖面。

Abstract: As an emerging operating system, HarmonyOS has a significant demand for software migration from platforms such as Android and iOS, where the User Interface (UI) translation accounts for a critical link. However, the latest UI development has shifted to declarative paradigms, e.g., Kotlin Jetpack Compose (KJC) for Android, SwiftUI for iOS, and ArkUI for HarmonyOS, rendering prior translation approaches inapplicable, as they target either backend logic or legacy imperative UIs. As such, this paper targets ArkUI and proposes an automatic translation approach, namely ArkTrans, to port UI files from Android and iOS to HarmonyOS. ArkTrans overcomes two salient challenges during the translation: (1) Programming Language (PL) unfamiliarity, and (2) severe syntactic chaos. Towards the first challenge, ArkTrans heuristically constructs ArkUI skeletons by extracting metadata from source PL, thereby guiding LLMs' initial translation. As for the second challenge, ArkTrans executes empirically revealed post-fixing rules via pattern matching to repair most of the remaining syntactic errors. To examine the effectiveness of ArkTrans, we construct a 100-sample parallel UI page translation benchmark from KJC/SwiftUI to ArkUI at the file level. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LLMs with direct/one-shot prompting cannot translate a single compilable UI page. In contrast, at most 90.67\% ArkTrans-translated files can be successfully compiled with high visual fidelity.

</details>


### [78] [SWE-Explore: Benchmarking How Coding Agents Explore Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07297)
*Shaoqiu Zhang,Yuhang Wang,Jialiang Liang,Yuling Shi,Wenhao Zeng,Maoquan Wang,Shilin He,Ningyuan Xu,Siyu Ye,Kai Cai,Xiaodong Gu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SWE-Explore 是一个用于独立评估编码智能体仓库探索能力的新基准，将仓库探索任务从端到端修复中解耦出来，定义为在固定行预算下返回与问题相关的代码区域排序列表。基准覆盖 848 个实例、10 种编程语言和 203 个开源仓库，通过轨迹派生的行级真值标注和多维度度量（覆盖率、排名、上下文效率）对各种检索器、通用编码智能体和专用定位器进行系统比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级编码基准（如 SWE-bench）通常将任务简化为二元的修复成功/失败预测，无法揭示智能体在仓库理解、上下文检索、代码定位和缺陷诊断等细粒度能力上的差异。论文指出智能体失败可能源于"未充分探索相关代码"或"检索到证据但未能合成正确补丁"，而前者被整体性指标所掩盖。因此需要一种能精确衡量行级仓库探索质量的独立评估目标。

Method: （1）任务形式化：将仓库探索定义为函数 f:(q,R)→P=(r1,...,rK)，返回包含文件路径和行范围的排序区域列表，K 固定为 5；（2）真值标注：从 GPT-5.4、Gemini-3-Pro、Sonnet-4.6、GLM-5.1、Kimi-K2.6 等强模型的成功解决轨迹中提取可观测的读动作（编辑器视图、命令行读取、grep 命中），通过跨轨迹交集获得保守核心区域 R_int，再经 LLM 精炼和人工审计生成最终 R_core；（3）多维度量：行级 Prec/Rec/F1、文件级和区域级 HitRate、预算感知的 nDCG@B、First Useful Hit (FUH)、Context Efficiency 和噪声率；（4）下游验证：构造受限上下文环境，将各探索器输出作为唯一可见上下文喂给固定修复智能体，用原始测试套件判定解决率，作为探索度量的外部效度检验；（5）受控上下文扰动实验，区分"缺失核心证据"与"冗余非核心上下文"对修复的影响。

Result: （1）探索度量与下游修复强相关：Context Efficiency 与解决率的 Pearson 相关性达 0.950，Rec@100 的 Spearman 相关性达 0.845，HitFile、HitRegion、FUH 均为强预测信号；（2）智能体式探索者明显优于经典检索：BM25、TF-IDF、Potion 等方法在各指标上接近 Random，而所有智能体式探索者大幅领先；（3）行级召回是关键瓶颈：现代智能体 HitFile 达 0.65–0.95，但行级 Rec_ℓ 仅 0.14–0.19，文件级定位已成熟而行级覆盖和高效排名仍为差异化关键；（4）通用编码智能体表现高度相似：Claude Code、Codex、OpenHands、Mini-SWE-Agent、AweAgent 在覆盖率、排名和效率上处于相近操作点；（5）专用定位器中仅 CoSIL 通过迭代代码图搜索显著提升行级召回至 0.788；（6）LLM 替换不消除瓶颈：GPT 系列最强但仍受行级召回限制；（7）缺失核心证据是主要失败模式：解决率在核心区域覆盖 50%→75% 间出现阈值式跃升，冗余上下文在高覆盖下可容忍；（8）案例研究在 scikit-learn#10844 实例上验证了上述排序和差距的全局一致性。

Conclusion: SWE-Explore 将仓库探索确立为可比较的评估目标，通过轨迹派生的行级监督在轻量级上下文选择任务下统一比较检索器、搜索智能体和长上下文选择器。实验证明探索度量有效追踪下游修复行为，揭示当前智能体在文件级定位已强但行级召回受限的关键瓶颈，并表明缺失核心证据比适度冗余更具破坏性。该基准为构建能更广泛读取仓库并暴露修复所需关键代码片段的探索器提供了明确的研究方向。

Abstract: Repository-level coding benchmarks such as SWE-bench have driven a rapid surge in the capabilities of coding agents. Yet they usually treat coding tasks as a holistic, binary prediction problem (e.g., resolved or unresolved), neglecting fine-grained agent capabilities such as repository understanding, context retrieval, code localization, and bug diagnosis. In this paper, we introduce SWE-Explore, a benchmark that isolates the evaluation of repository exploration, a critical capability of coding agents. Given a repository and an issue, SWE-Explore asks an explorer to return a ranked list of relevant code regions under a fixed line budget. SWE-Explore covers 848 issues across 10 programming languages and 203 open-source repositories. For each instance, we derive line-level ground truth from independent agent trajectories that successfully solved the same issue, distilling the specific code regions their solution paths actually consulted. We evaluate exploration along coverage, ranking, and context-efficiency dimensions, showing that these metrics strongly track downstream repair behavior. Across a broad set of retrieval methods, general coding agents, and specialized localizers, we find that agentic explorers form a clear tier above classical retrieval. While file-level localization is already strong for modern methods, line-level coverage and efficient ranking remain the key axes differentiating state-of-the-art explorers.

</details>


### [79] [QBugLM: An Agentic Benchmarking Framework for LLM-based Quantum Software Debugging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07314)
*An B. B. Pham,Hoa T. Nguyen,Muhammad Usman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: QBugLM 是一个面向量子软件调试的多智能体基准测试框架，针对与 SDK 无关的 OpenQASM 3.0 程序，实现从基于分类法的缺陷注入、LLM 检测与修复到基于模拟验证的端到端自动化流水线；案例研究显示迭代反馈至关重要（一次重试即可将 Pass@1 从低于 25% 提升至超过 80%），结构化提示在固定资源约束下优于 CoT 和 ReAct，且开源模型 Qwen3 Coder Next 在多数缺陷类别上以更低的成本达到与 Claude Sonnet 4.6 相当的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件缺陷常产生静默错误而非显式异常，传统调试手段难以处理；尽管 LLM 在经典软件工程任务上表现强劲，但其在量子代码调试（特别是与 SDK 无关的 OpenQASM 3.0 程序）方面的能力尚未被系统研究。现有研究多聚焦于特定 SDK（如 Qiskit）的代码生成，缺少对检测-修复闭环、提示策略与多轮迭代效果的端到端基准评估框架。

Method: 框架包含四个组件：(1) QBugGen 基于四类缺陷分类法（C1 已弃用语法、C2 电路结构错误、C3 门过度使用/冗余、C4 语义偏差）对 MQT Bench 的 OpenQASM 3.0 基准程序进行单缺陷注入；(2) QBugFind 由 LLM 智能体生成结构化缺陷报告（位置+类别）；(3) QBugFix 由独立的 LLM 智能体执行修复；(4) QBugCheck 通过无噪声量子模拟器比较测量分布的总变差距离（εδ=0.05）和门数差异（εg=0）进行确定性验证。流水线采用 Strands Agents 编排，最多 3 次尝试（K=3），将历史反馈注入后续迭代。案例研究针对两个 LLM（Claude Sonnet 4.6、Qwen3 Coder Next）、三种提示策略（Structured/CoT/ReAct）和五种 5-qubit 电路（DJ、Grover、BV、GHZ、W）进行评估，使用无偏 Pass@k 估计器、Token 消耗、墙钟时间和 AWS Bedrock 货币成本作为指标。

Result: RQ1：结构化提示在 BV 电路上对两模型均取得最高 Pass@1（Sonnet 4.6 达 97%，Qwen3 达 95%），均优于 CoT（90%/45%）和 ReAct（95%/63%）。RQ2：无重试时 Qwen3 在 C1/C3/C4 上仅 20%，Sonnet 4.6 在这三类上完全失败；结构错误（C2）是唯一单次可部分可靠的类别（两模型均 60%）；经两次重试后，Pass@5 在所有类别上达 100%，但两模型各自保留一个未解决的弱点（Sonnet 4.6 在 C2 仅 80%，Qwen3 在 C4 仅 92%）。RQ3：在 C1/C2/C3 上，Qwen3 以 4.8-9.1 倍更低的成本和 1.5-4.6 倍更快的速度达到与 Sonnet 4.6 相当或更高的 Pass@1；但 C4 例外，Qwen3 消耗约 350K tokens（成本 $0.198）却仅取得 92% Pass@1，而 Sonnet 4.6 消耗 91K tokens 即达 100%。

Conclusion: QBugLM 提供了首个面向量子软件调试的端到端多智能体基准框架，在与 SDK 无关的 OpenQASM 3.0 上实现了缺陷生成、检测、修复与验证的全自动化。三大发现：(1) 迭代反馈是修复成功的主导因素，一次重试即可使 Pass@1 从低于 25% 跃升至超过 80%，但后续重试收益递减；(2) 对于本身具备推理能力的模型，更简单的结构化提示在固定资源约束下反而优于 CoT 和 ReAct；(3) 开源 Qwen3 Coder Next 在多数缺陷类型上以显著更低的成本提供与商业 Sonnet 4.6 相当的准确率，但语义偏差类缺陷仍需采用按缺陷类型感知的部署策略。未来工作将扩展缺陷语料（多缺陷注入）、扩展到更大电路与多框架、探索混合 finder/fixer 智能体配置及 RAG 与文档增强技术，并与静态/动态/形式化分析方法进行对比。

Abstract: Quantum software bugs often yield silent, incorrect outputs rather than explicit errors, making them particularly difficult to detect and repair with conventional techniques. Although large language models (LLMs) have shown strong performance on classical software engineering tasks, their ability to debug quantum code remains largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose QBugLM, a multi-agent framework that automates the quantum software debugging pipeline, from taxonomy-driven bug injection to LLM-based detection and repair, and finally to simulation-based validation, for framework-agnostic OpenQASM 3.0 programs. We further conduct a comprehensive case study using QBugLM to benchmark two LLMs, Claude 4.6 Sonnet and Qwen3 Coder Next, across different prompting strategies, bug categories, and quantum programs. Our results show that iterative feedback is critical, as a single retry raises Pass@1 from below 25% to above 80%. Moreover, simpler structured prompting can even outperform Chain-of-Thought and ReAct for reasoning-capable models under fixed-resource constraints. Our work takes initial steps toward benchmarking LLM capabilities for debugging quantum programs and offers practical insights to support future efforts in automated quantum software repair.

</details>


### [80] [Is US Defense Acquisition Ready to Acquire AI-Enabled Capabilities? Assessing the DoD Software Acquisition Pathway Through a Scenario-Based Policy Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07393)
*Daniel Lugo,James C. Davis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过政策情景分析(PSA)方法,评估美国国防部软件采办路径(SWP)及其治理体系是否足以支持AI能力的采办。研究发现,SWP为AI采办提供了可行基础,但在数据治理、生命周期管理、网络安全可追溯性和人工监督等方面存在政策到产物的"可操作性鸿沟"。作者建议在SWP内设立AI支持性子路径,并对相关规划产物进行针对性细化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统从实验原型转变为关键任务工具,其对动态数据、演化的模型和治理的依赖,引发了现有采办路径能否跟上步伐的质疑。美国国防部通过适应性采办框架(AAF)对采办流程进行了现代化改造,其中软件采办路径(SWP)是采办软件密集型能力的主要机制。本文旨在评估SWP是否足以应对AI采办的独特需求。

Method: 采用政策情景分析(Policy Scenario Analysis, PSA)方法,构建一个虚拟的"目标定位AI"基线场景和一个传统的"飞行规划优化"对照场景,使用三阶段评估流程(参数定义、政策语料选择、情景构建;情景映射SWP规划活动;生成覆盖评分卡和建议报告)。通过五个情景剧集(数据获取、TEVV设计、网络安全与发布溯源、生命周期管理、人工监督约束)和五个评估关切(数据治理、保证与T&E、网络安全与可追溯性、生命周期管理、人工监督),使用"明确/部分/缺失"的三元编码方案评估治理体系的覆盖程度。

Result: 研究结果显示:SWP为AI密集型软件项目的高层需求提供了可行基础,但其可操作性变得不均衡。具体发现包括:(1)数据治理与数据权利——覆盖为"部分",缺乏对训练数据来源、标注质量、版本控制等的明确规定;(2)AI保证与TEVV——覆盖为"明确",SWP测试结构结合CDAO AI T&E指南直接支持;(3)网络安全与可追溯性——覆盖为"部分",缺乏端到端可追溯性的发布级要求;(4)生命周期管理——覆盖为"部分",缺乏漂移检测、再训练触发因素和AI特定性能阈值的标准化;(5)人工监督——覆盖为"部分",RAI原则未嵌入到具体的路径产物中。研究识别出的核心问题是"政策到产物的鸿沟":AI相关控制分散在补充文件中,而非嵌入到程序执行机制中,导致项目办公室依赖不一致的本地解读。

Conclusion: 本文得出结论:当前的治理结构为采办AI能力提供了可行基础,但在将高层AI政策转化为面向项目的采办实践方面仍不均衡。核心弱点是政策与产物之间的差距——AI相关控制虽然存在于更广泛的治理体系中,但未一致地转化为有效管理SWP项目所需的产物、内容期望和审查逻辑。建议通过在SWP内建立AI支持性子路径(为AI能力规划提供机构归属),并实施针对性的产物细化(包括AI数据附件、基于模型的产物支持策略、集成网络安全和人工监督)来弥合这一差距。

Abstract: As AI systems transition from experimental prototypes to mission-critical tools, their dependence on dynamic data, evolving models, and governance raises questions about whether existing acquisition pathways can keep pace. The U.S. Department of Defense has modernized its acquisition processes through the Adaptive Acquisition Framework, with the Software Acquisition Pathway (SWP) serving as the primary mechanism for acquiring software-intensive capabilities. This paper evaluates whether SWP is sufficient to address the unique demands of AI acquisition. In this work, we perform a scenario-based evaluation that traces a notional AI-enabled program through key SWP planning activities to assess how policy translates into program artifacts and decisions. We use Policy Scenario Analysis to examine whether the SWP-centered governance stack provides sufficient actionable support for AI acquisition. The governance stack provides a viable foundation for iterative delivery and AI testing. However, we identify a recurring actionability problem in the core guidance. AI-specific controls for data provenance, lifecycle management, and human oversight remain distributed across supplemental documents rather than embedded in the program-facing mechanisms through which SWP is executed. This disconnect leaves program offices reliant on inconsistent local interpretation. We conclude by recommending an AI-supporting sub-path and targeted artifact refinements to better bridge this policy-to-artifact gap.

</details>


### [81] [Agentic Very Much! Adoption of Coding Agent in New GitHub Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07448)
*Romain Robbes,Théo Matricon,Thomas Degueule,Andre Hora,Stefano Zacchiroli*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是先前关于GitHub项目中编码智能体（coding agents）采用情况研究的续作。研究发现，在新创建的GitHub项目中，编码智能体的采用率比之前研究高出两倍以上，且AI辅助提交的比例显著更高，表明采用强度也在增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI编码工具（如GitHub Copilot、ChatGPT等）的快速发展，其在软件开发中的实际采用情况和影响程度是软件工程研究的重要议题。本文是作者先前研究的延伸，旨在追踪编码智能体在GitHub项目中采用情况的最新变化趋势，了解AI辅助开发在真实项目中的普及程度。

Method: 本文采用了纵向对比研究方法：1) 基于先前已有的研究框架；2) 将样本范围扩展到先前研究之后新创建的GitHub项目；3) 对比新旧样本中编码智能体的采用率；4) 测量AI辅助提交在总提交中的比例，以评估采用的强度；5) 讨论了检测方法的局限性。

Result: 1) 新样本中编码智能体的采用率比先前研究高出两倍以上；2) AI辅助提交在总提交中的占比显著提高，表明采用强度明显增强；3) 存在明显迹象表明当前检测方法可能未能捕捉到所有AI辅助的提交，实际采用率可能更高。

Conclusion: 编码智能体在GitHub项目中的采用正在快速增长，无论是采用范围（采用项目的数量）还是采用深度（单个项目中AI辅助提交的占比）都在显著提升。该趋势表明AI辅助编程已成为软件开发的新常态，但由于检测技术的限制，实际影响可能比观测到的还要深远。未来的研究方向可能包括改进检测方法以及探讨AI编码工具对软件质量和开发流程的具体影响。

Abstract: In previous work, we investigated the adoption of coding agents in GitHub projects, finding that it was very significant. This study follows this line of work, but analyses new projects, that were created after the previous study. In this new sample, we find that the adoption of coding agents is more than twice as high. We also find that the adoption is significantly more intensive, as the proportion of AI-assisted commits is sensibly higher, despite strong signs that we do not detect all of it.

</details>

### [82] [AI Sovereignty: A Qualitative Model of Strategic Competition as AI Becomes an Instrument of National Power](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07245)
*Timothy Clancy,Asmeret Naugle*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了"AI 主权"（AI sovereignty）的定义与概念框架，构建了涵盖微观、中观和宏观三个层次的定性模型，用于分析各国在 AI 领域竞争与国家权力博弈中的关键杠杆点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿 AI 模型的竞争对国家战略意义日益凸显，但 AI 主权的实现成本巨大且缺乏清晰的定义与概念模型，难以有效应对不断演化的 AI 主权动态。

Method: 通过提出 AI 主权的相关定义，并首创性地构建了一个包含微观、中观和宏观三个层次的定性概念模型，对关键杠杆点进行基于模型的情景预测分析。

Result: 模型识别出加速器、电力、水资源、数据集和熟练劳动力等关键杠杆点，并揭示这些杠杆点可通过动能直接行动（如伊朗用无人机攻击数据中心）以及非动能间接影响（网络、太空、信息、经济胁迫和外交）在战略和作战层面被激活，从而增强本国实力或削弱对手。

Conclusion: 如果假设成立，AI 主权的战略竞争将成为 21 世纪各国提升经济实力、竞争优势和整体国家力量的决定性因素，AI 主权将重塑国际竞争格局。

Abstract: AI sovereignty is the extent to which a nation independently controls its artificial intelligence (AI) technologies. The race toward ever-more-sophisticated frontier AI models is of increasing strategic importance, with nations considering how AI might improve their economic situations, competitive advantage, and overall national power. However, the costs of AI sovereignty are enormous, and we lack definitions and conceptual models to navigate evolving AI sovereignty dynamics. We address this gap with definitions relevant to AI sovereignty, along with a first-of-its-kind qualitative model that incorporates micro, meso, and macro contributors. Model-based qualitative forecasts highlight competitive dynamics and evolving potential for AI-driven national power. The model identifies key leverage points that nations can use to enhance their own growth or degrade an adversary's, including consideration of accelerators, electricity, water, data sets and skilled workforce. These leverage points can be activated at strategic and operational levels through both direct kinetic actions, such as Iran's targeting of data centers with drones, and indirect non-kinetic effects including cyber, space, information, economic coercion and diplomacy. If our assumptions and hypotheses are valid, this strategic competition may come to define how nations improve their economic situations, competitive advantage, and overall national power in the 21st Century.

</details>


### [83] [Learning Fair Demand Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06830)
*Adam N. Elmachtoub,Hyemi Kim,Jonathan Y. Tan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究数据驱动定价中的公平性问题，构建了一个两阶段定价管道（需求估计+价格优化），比较了两种公平性实施框架——Fair-Estimate-then-Optimize (FEO) 与 Estimate-then-Fair-Optimize (EFO)，分析了在预测损失、价格、需求三类公平对象与均等/Rawlsian两种规范视角下的策略选择与下游社会福利权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动定价在航空、信贷、保险、零售等领域日益普及，但基于消费者特征学习需求模型并据此定价可能产生歧视性结果，引发公平性争议。现有研究多关注预测阶段的公平性，但未解决三个核心问题：应采用何种公平性概念？应在管道的哪个阶段施加公平性？不同选择如何影响社会福利？

Method: 构建两群体、线性需求模型的风格化设定：(1) 需求估计阶段使用MSE损失函数最小二乘估计价格敏感系数 $b_g$；(2) 优化阶段基于估计模型最大化利润。提出FEO（在估计阶段施加公平性约束的双层优化）与EFO（先无偏估计再在优化阶段施加公平性约束）两种框架。考虑三类公平对象（损失、价格、需求）×两类规范视角（parity-wise、Rawlsian）。通过KKT条件与一阶/二阶Taylor展开分析小公平水平 $\alpha \to 0$ 时的局部行为，并扩展至带特征的线性与logistic需求模型。最后基于瑞典蜱传脑炎疫苗接种调查数据进行实证。

Result: 核心发现：(1) **损失公平性存在多解问题**（命题3.1）：parity-wise损失公平存在两个解 $b_0^{\pm}(\alpha)$，对消费者剩余和社会福利有相反影响；当解被随机选取时，期望社会福利随 $\alpha$ 增大而下降（推论3.2）。(2) **parity-wise价格/需求公平下FEO与EFO存在阈值**（命题3.3）：定义平衡指标 $\tau_p = (a_0^2 \bar{p_0^2}/(a_1^2 \bar{p_1^2}))^{1/3}$，当 $(\tau_p - \tau_b)(\tau_a - \tau_b) > 0$ 时FEO对消费者更优（价格公平），反之EFO更优（需求公平）。在市场容量相近、数据价格分布相似时，价格公平下FEO占优，需求公平下EFO占优（推论3.4）。(3) **利润比较**（命题3.5）：在存在估计误差时，FEO中的公平约束可起到正则化作用，特定条件下FEO利润高于EFO。(4) **Rawlsian公平下FEO与EFO结果一致**（命题3.6）：且在特定条件下公平性可同时提升利润与消费者剩余，实现双赢。(5) 案例研究（TBE疫苗）显示在parity-wise需求公平下EFO严格优于FEO，其他标准下无支配关系。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了数据驱动定价中公平性实施位置的关键作用：公平性干预的阶段选择（估计 vs 优化）对利润、消费者剩余和社会福利有实质性影响。研究表明，(i) 损失公平性因多解性可能产生不良后果，需在估计时考虑下游效应；(ii) 市场需求和价格分布是决定parity-wise公平下策略选择的关键因素；(iii) Rawlsian公平可在某些条件下实现公平与效率的双赢。政策启示在于：监管重点（保护利润 vs 最大化消费者剩余）决定了应在算法定价管道的何处实施公平性干预。未来研究方向包括扩展到更丰富的需求函数、多群体结构，以及数据统计性质与公平约束的交互作用。

Abstract: Data-driven pricing is increasingly prevalent in sectors such as airlines, lending, insurance, and retail. By learning demand models from customer features and setting prices accordingly, these systems may generate discriminatory outcomes that raise fairness concerns. This leads to fundamental questions - how and where should systems incorporate fairness considerations in the pricing pipeline, and how does it ultimately affect societal outcomes? To answer these, we study a stylized model where a seller has a two-stage decision pipeline comprising linear demand model estimation followed by price optimization. The seller considers fairness notions in training loss, price, and demand, under both parity-wise and Rawlsian perspectives. We show that equalizing training loss across consumer groups leads to multiple solutions, which in turn can result in undesirable outcomes despite being a standard approach in fair machine learning. Focusing instead on fairness applied directly to prices or demand, we compare two strategies that enforce fairness in either the demand estimation stage or the price optimization stage. For parity-wise fairness, we characterize when each strategy yields higher social welfare under small fairness levels. We show that when market sizes and prices in the dataset are similar, imposing price fairness in the estimation stage is more beneficial to consumers, whereas imposing demand fairness in the optimization stage yields better consumer outcomes. For Rawlsian fairness, the two strategies coincide exactly. Lastly, we extend our model to alternate demand functions and conduct a case study using real-world vaccine pricing data.

</details>


### [84] [Toward a Metaphysics of Learning Analytics: Ontological Positioning of Data, Inference, and Normativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06851)
*Kensuke Takii*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该论文为学习分析（LA）领域建立了一套描述性形而上学体系。基于Strawson的描述性形而上学方法，从LA自身的定义中推导出"零号原则"（学习者是LA的本体论前提）和"第一原则"（关于学习者及其学习的"形式化构成记录"是LA推论的唯一合法基础），识别出LA中涉及的八类主体，并提出"规范嵌入式LA"概念，揭示其将LA目的与操作混同所引发的本体论张力，最终论证LA无法从事实数据中推导规范性命题（休谟的"是/应当"问题）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习分析（LA）领域自2011年首个定义确立以来已发展15年，但相对于认识论和伦理学的活跃讨论，其形而上学（尤其是本体论）层面的反思极为匮乏。这种缺失阻碍了LA建立自身的学术与实践基础，也使基于其上的各种活动缺乏身份认同和解释力。论文试图填补这一空白，从LA自身的定义与原则出发，回答"LA是什么"这一本体论问题。

Method: 采用Strawson（1959）的描述性形而上学方法——不是从外部强加更好的理想形式，而是揭示LA实践中已隐含的概念结构及其本体论承诺。从SoLAR的LA定义出发，通过逻辑推演提炼出"零号原则"、"第一原则"，界定"形式化构成记录"作为数据的精确定义，识别LA中的八类主体（学习者、系统设计者、数据收集者、数据分析者、数据解释者、数据传播者、决策者、知识翻译者），并通过休谟的"是/应当"问题分析规范判断在LA中的地位。

Result: 1. 确立"零号原则"：学习者是LA的本体论前提（因为数据源于学习者及其学习）；2. 确立"第一原则"：LA推论的最小合法基础是关于学习者及其学习的"形式化构成记录"，LA无法从中逻辑地推导出规范性命题；3. 识别出八类主体及其在LA中的角色与责任；4. 提出"规范嵌入式LA"概念，揭示该类实践通过将"理想学习状态"的规范命题作为数据收集、分析或解释的先验约束，与第一原则产生本体论张力；5. 通过将规范判断定位在LA的"决策者"外部（类似地图与目的地的关系），既维护了LA的逻辑纯洁性，也保障了规范判断的独立性；6. 论证了教育数据挖掘、学习者建模、学习设计等相邻领域与LA的本体论关系。

Conclusion: 该论文构建的形而上学体系并非限制或否定现有LA实践，而是通过分离认识论、价值论和本体论讨论的层次，为这些讨论提供独立空间。其核心主张是：LA的认识权威源于"形式化构成记录"，任何在该推论之前嵌入规范判断的实践都在LA自身的本体论条件之外运行。该框架的价值在于使LA实践长期隐含的本体论结构变得显式、系统化且在哲学上可辩护，为LA社区正在进行的批判性对话提供稳定的参考点和起点。

Abstract: The Learning Analytics (LA) community has undergone rapid development over the 15 years since the first LAK conference was held. However, while epistemological and ethical debates regarding the philosophical foundations of LA have been vigorous, metaphysical discussions have been sparse, signifying a lack of effort to derive the identity of LA from its internal principles. In this paper, we attempt to establish a metaphysics of LA by addressing the ontological question of ``What is LA?'' We do so by tracing back to LA's own definitions and principles to derive an answer from within LA itself. Specifically, we address what kind of existence the data LA operates on constitutes, identify eight agents including learners as ontological prerequisites, and clarify, via the is/ought problem, that LA does not derive norms from data. In particular, this system reveals that a class of LA practices, here termed \textit{norm-embedded LA}, conflates LA's purpose with its operations, creating an ontological tension with the first principle. We also discuss connections with related fields and the limitations of this system. The metaphysics outlined here is not imposed from outside LA, but surfaces what LA itself has always implicitly presupposed.

</details>


### [85] [Two-Phase Simulated Annealing for Equitable Team Formation: Eliminating Complaints in Large Engineering Cohorts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07270)
*Yiwei Sun,Xinru Deng,Dimitrios G Papageorgiou*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段算法框架,第一阶段通过图论聚类形成满足偏好三元组,第二阶段利用模拟退火将三元组配对为六人团队,在238名工程学生中部署后实现了零投诉、GPA方差降至0.005、消除性别隔离个体,并保持94.3%的偏好满意度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模工程课程(100+学生)的团队组建面临学生偏好、学术公平与人口多样性之间的多目标权衡困境。现有工具(CATME、Team-Anneal)侧重公平而忽略偏好,自选组方式则损害公平性,导致投诉率高达5–35%。该研究旨在填补偏好满足与公平约束难以兼得的空白。

Method: 两阶段分解策略:Phase 1构建有向偏好图,通过四层优先级层次分配(完全三角形→互选对+兼容第三成员→单向偏好→GPA均衡余项)形成固定三元组;Phase 2以模拟退火将三元组配对为六人团队,代价函数加权GPA方差(w₁=1000)、性别隔离数(w₂=20)与规模偏差(w₃=5),采用指数降温(T₀=100, α=0.995)与Metropolis接受准则。配套实施70%个人/30%小组的混合评分机制与版本控制贡献追踪以抑制搭便车行为。

Result: 238名学生(119材料+119高分子)部署结果显示:正式投诉率从>30%降至0%;团队间GPA方差从9.74±14.32降至0.005;完全消除性别隔离个体;94.3%偏好满意度;单次运行3–5秒。Materials队列发现18个完全互选三角形(聚类系数0.51 vs. 随机0.08),验证了工程学生中显著的传递性友谊网络。基于82个历史分组实例(1538个团队,六年数据)基线对比,所有指标均实现统计显著改进。

Conclusion: 偏好满足与公平优化可通过问题分解而非妥协式多目标优化同时实现。两阶段架构(局部结构→全局优化)类比材料处理中的分层优化原理,具有教学迁移价值——模拟退火作为材料科学核心概念被巧妙融入行政流程,转化为教学契机。局限包括:偏好提交率需>60%以保证效果;O(n³)最坏复杂度限制超大规模应用;特定中英联合培养文化背景可能影响普适性。未来方向涵盖动态团队调整、跨课程一致性组队、技能匹配与机器学习增强偏好预测。

Abstract: Contribution: This paper presents a novel two-phase algorithmic approach that decouples preference satisfaction from fairness optimization in student team formation, achieving both objectives without compromise. The method applies simulated annealing -- a core materials science technique -- to an educational challenge, demonstrating pedagogical integration of administrative processes. Background: Forming effective teams in large engineering cohorts (100+ students) requires balancing student preferences, academic fairness, and demographic diversity. Existing tools either optimize for fairness while ignoring preferences (CATME, Team-Anneal) or accommodate preferences while compromising balance (self-selection), leaving complaint rates at 5--35%. Intended Outcomes: Eliminate formal complaints, achieve near-zero GPA variance between teams, prevent gender isolation, and maintain high preference satisfaction while creating a scalable, reproducible solution applicable across engineering programs. Application Design: Phase 1 forms fixed triads through graph-theoretic clustering that maximizes mutual preferences, preserving social bonds. Phase 2 employs simulated annealing to pair triads into teams of six while optimizing GPA variance, gender balance, and size constraints. This decomposition mirrors hierarchical optimization in materials processing. Findings: Deployed across 238 students, the algorithm eliminated formal complaints entirely (vs >30% baseline), achieved GPA variance of 0.005 (vs. historical mean 9.74), eliminated gender-isolated individuals, and maintained 94.3% preference satisfaction. Validation against 82 historical grouping instances (1,538 teams, 6 academic years) confirmed significant improvement over conventional methods.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

### [86] [Mind the Gap: Bridging Behavioral Silos with LLMs in Multi-Vertical Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06779)
*Nimesh Sinha,Raghav Saboo,Martin Wang,Sudeep Das*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对DoorDash等多垂直电商平台中新型垂直领域（如杂货、零售）面临的冷启动用户个性化推荐难题，提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的跨域知识迁移框架。该框架利用分层检索增强生成（RAG）pipeline从用户丰富的餐饮订单历史和搜索查询中合成稀疏、高维的用户偏好特征，并将其集成到生产环境中的多任务学习（MTL）排序模型中，从而有效缓解新垂直领域的行为数据稀疏问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多垂直电商平台在拓展杂货、零售等新兴业务时，面临严重的冷启动问题：新垂直领域缺乏用户交互数据，难以提供个性化推荐。然而，平台在成熟垂直领域（如餐饮）积累了大量用户行为数据。如何将这些数据丰富垂直领域中的潜在用户偏好信号有效迁移到数据稀疏的新垂直领域，是亟待解决的核心问题。

Method: 1. 设计分层RAG pipeline：利用LLM（如GPT-4o-mini）将用户非结构化的餐饮订单记录和搜索查询映射到四级产品分类法（L1-L4），通过级联推理逐层细化分类，避免幻觉问题。2. 构造两类新特征：长期跨垂直偏好画像（来自历史订单）和短期意图画像（来自搜索行为）。3. 提示工程优化：设置temperature=0.1、置信度阈值0.8、按时间排序用户历史等机制提高生成质量。4. 特征集成：采用均值池化处理变长分类ID，嵌入到MTL排序模型的增强用户向量中。5. 成本优化：通过prompt缓存和按需更新策略降低约80%计算开销。

Result: 离线评估：整体用户群AUC-ROC提升4.4%，MRR提升4.8%；冷启动用户群AUC-ROC提升4.0%；高活跃用户群搜索信号贡献最大，AUC-ROC提升5.2%。在线影子部署：整体AUC-ROC提升4.3%，MRR提升3.2%。人工评估与LLM-as-a-judge评估均显示搜索查询生成的特征个性化程度更高。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了LLM作为语义桥梁在跨域知识迁移中的有效性，特别是在缓解冷启动问题上效果显著。未来工作方向包括：探索更高级的提示范式（CoT、自我纠错）、对小型LLM进行领域微调、将生成特征集成到召回阶段、以及建模偏好的时间动态性以构建会话感知的推荐系统。

Abstract: In multi-vertical e-commerce platforms like DoorDash, relatively newer product verticals such as grocery and retail present a significant opportunity for personalization innovation. A key challenge lies in solving the "cold start" problem for users. This paper introduces a novel framework for enhancing recommendation quality by transferring knowledge from data-rich verticals (e.g., restaurants at DoorDash) to data-sparse ones. We leverage Large Language Models (LLMs) to perform generative inference, synthesizing sparse, high-dimensional features that encapsulate latent user affinities. Specifically, we employ a hierarchical Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipeline to derive multi-level taxonomic features from user restaurant order histories and search queries. These generated features, encoding both long-term cross-vertical preferences and short-term intent, are integrated into a production Multi-Task Learning (MTL) ranking model. We demonstrate through extensive offline and online evaluation that this approach significantly improves personalization and engagement in emerging business verticals, effectively bridging the behavioral data gap.

</details>


### [87] [PaperFlow: Profiling, Recommending, and Adapting Across Daily Paper Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07454)
*Fuqiang Wang,Song Tan,Zheng Guo,Jiaohao Fu,Xinglong Xu,Bihui Yu,Jie Dong,Zheng Sun,Siyuan Li,Jingxuan Wei,Cheng Tan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PaperFlow 是一个面向科学论文推荐的纵向（longitudinal）每日阅读循环框架，包含 Profiling（结构化学者画像构建）、Recommending（多信号每日 Top-20 排序）和 Adapting（基于语义异构反馈与兴趣漂移的状态更新）三个耦合阶段。作者构建了一个新的纵向用户-日基准（24 个模拟研究者、50 天、1,200 个 user-day episodes、20,727 篇论文、497,448 条记录），并配套盲评人工评估协议。在与 5 个基线的对比中，PaperFlow 在 oracle 排序、行为对齐和人工评估上均取得最优，并分析了静态相关性与动态自适应之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学论文推荐通常被建模为一次性静态排序（固定候选集、单次打分），忽略了科研阅读的时序性：研究人员每天面对新论文流、阅读时间稀缺、兴趣会随项目推进而漂移、反馈不断累积。需要一个能将画像维护、每日受限预算下的排序、反馈驱动状态更新统一在单一时间闭环中的框架和可复现的纵向评测基准。

Method: 框架层面：1) Profiling：维护可编辑、可审查的结构化学者画像 p_t，分离研究方向 D/T、作者/机构/必读先验 A/I/M、报告偏好 Q、阅读行为 B 和漂移状态 d；2) Recommending：对当日候选池 C_t 聚合多信号打分 S_match + S_prior + S_dyn + S_must，按 must_read/high_relevant/maybe_interested/edge_relevant 四档展示，受固定 Top-20 预算约束；3) Adapting：按反馈语义差异化更新（选择/编辑/深度阅读为强证据，跳过为弱负证据，报告反馈仅更新 Q），并使用基于行为的兴趣漂移模块 g_t = Drift(L_t, S_t, F_t, B_t) 在四个状态（Stable/Observing/Shifting/Recovered）间转换，每轮主题权重变化受 ϵ 上界约束。基准层面：冻结 arXiv 每日候选池、隐藏同日反馈和未来信息、固定池设置以隔离方法差异。评估层面：oracle 排序指标（gNDCG@20、OracleRecall@20、MRR@20 等汇总为 RecommendationScore）、行为对齐指标 SelectedNDCG@20，以及三套盲评人工分数（HumanEval、ModelHumanScore、AdaptationHumanScore）。

Result: 在主实验中，PaperFlow 的 gNDCG@20=50.65、RecommendationScore=55.31、HumanEval=65.56，均优于最强基线 Scholar Inbox（分别为 39.00/46.30/55.56），SelectedNDCG@20 从 33.47 提升到 70.88，表明行为对齐改善尤为显著。LLM 骨干对比（14 个模型）中，ModelAutoScore 与 ModelHumanScore 高度相关（Pearson r=0.9632），Grok 4.3 在闭源中得分与成本综合最优。消融实验揭示了静态-动态权衡：去掉画像更新/漂移/阅读信号后，部分 oracle 指标反而上升，但 SelectedNDCG@20 降低（Full 0.7088 vs 简化变体 0.6659-0.7074）。漂移分析中 PaperFlow 取得最高 PostDrift SelectedNDCG@20、NewTopicRecall@20 和最低 OldTopicRate@20，DriftAutoScore=72.76、AdaptationHumanScore=68.75。真实用户试点（5 名 CS 研究生、5-7 轮）显示 PaperFlow 的 Prec@5=0.71、ReadRate=0.34、满意度 4.0/5，优于每日 arXiv 邮件和静态画像基线。

Conclusion: PaperFlow 将科学论文推荐从一次性排序重构为"画像-推荐-自适应"的纵向每日闭环，并通过冻结候选池、隐藏 oracle 标签和盲评协议定义了可复现的纵向评测协议。实验证明该框架在排序质量、行为对齐、兴趣漂移适应和人工评价上均优于现有方法，但也揭示了静态相关性与动态行为一致性之间的固有权衡。主要局限在于以模拟用户和模拟相关性标签为主、且数据来源以 arXiv 为主，未来需扩展到更大规模人工评估、更多学术来源与部署研究。

Abstract: Scientific paper recommendation is typically evaluated as static ranking over a fixed candidate set, yet real scientific reading unfolds as a daily, longitudinal process in which interests shift and feedback accumulates. We introduce PaperFlow, a framework that organizes it into three coupled stages: Profiling, which constructs and maintains a structured, inspectable scholarly profile from heterogeneous cold-start evidence; Recommending, which ranks each date-specific paper stream through multi-signal aggregation under a fixed display budget; and Adapting, which updates user state from semantically distinct feedback signals and models interest drift across days. We further define a longitudinal user-day benchmark that fixes users, dates, candidate pools, visible inputs, and hidden simulated relevance labels under a shared temporal information boundary. The benchmark contains 24 simulated research users, 50 daily paper streams, 1,200 user-day episodes, 20,727 unique papers, and 497,448 episode-paper records. We additionally specify a blind human-evaluation protocol to validate alignment between automatic metrics and expert judgments. Experiments against five scientific recommendation baselines show that PaperFlow achieves the strongest oracle-based ranking, the highest behavioral alignment with simulated reading selections, and the best blind human-evaluation score.

</details>


### [88] [FLOWREADER: Min-Cost Flow Optimization for Multi-Modal Long Document Q&A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07235)
*Ambuj Mehrish,Sebatiano Vascon*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: FlowReader 是一种面向多模态长文档问答的检索增强框架，将证据组装重新建模为多模态节点图上的最小费用流问题，通过统一的评分向量控制源/汇选择与边成本/容量，并结合熵正则化复制者动力学与双过程门控机制，在碎片化证据场景下显著优于传统 top-k 检索方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 系统在处理多模态长文档时存在三大瓶颈：(1) 检索粒度与真实证据粒度不匹配，文本分块会切断跨表格、幻灯片、图文等碎片化证据；(2) 独立打分的片段无法建模结构性与多跳依赖；(3) 固定检索策略对不同复杂度查询一视同仁。当证据分散于表格单元格、多张幻灯片或图文之间时，top-k 检索失效。

Method: 方法分为三个阶段：(1) 离线构建多模态图 G=(V,E)，节点包含文本/视觉/表格三种模态属性（摘要、关键词、嵌入）。(2) 查询时计算每个节点的综合得分 h_i = λ_r·r_i + λ_ϕ·ϕ_i + λ_ψ·ψ_i，其中 r_i 融合密集检索与 BM25，ϕ_i 为沿图结构的前向传播得分，ψ_i 为个性化 PageRank 长程扩散得分。(3) 在图上求解最小费用流 LP：源点由 MMR 选取的多样化查询锚点构成，汇点由答案性得分 a_i = r_i·ρ_i 决定，边成本和容量均由 h 控制。流分解为候选证据链后，通过熵正则化复制者动力学选择紧凑非冗余子集；最后由并行 VLM 工作器生成答案，由双过程门控在答案一致性低或流饱和度不足时触发单次 System-2 精炼。

Result: 在 VisDoMBench 五个子集上的评估显示：FlowReader 在证据碎片化最严重的两个子集上达到最优——PaperTab 58.40（比 G²-Reader 高 1.30 分）和 SlideVQA 72.93（高 0.62 分），在 SPIQA、FetaTab、SciGraphQA 上具有竞争力。宏平均 65.47，与最强基线 G²-Reader（66.21）仅差 0.74 分。消融实验验证了各组件的必要性：去除熵正则化导致 FetaTab 下降 5.60 分，去除前向传播导致 SlideVQA 下降 2.33 分，System 2 门控在多跳视觉查询上带来 1.19-1.43 分增益。同时 FlowReader 具有有界的 VLM 调用预算（15.6-47.3 次），而 G²-Reader 无上界。

Conclusion: FlowReader 证明最小费用流在碎片化多模态证据场景下优于 top-k 检索，且提供了一种统一控制评分、路由、选择与自适应计算的方式。局限包括：单向量 h 缺乏独立校正机制、流预算 F 对所有查询固定、熵正则化复制者动力学缺乏形式化最大团保证。未来方向包括：在 System 2 门下学习图结构、从答案监督中学习边成本与路径质量指数、以及将 F 设为查询依赖变量。

Abstract: Long, multimodal documents force retrieval-augmented systems to assemble answers from evidence fragmented across text, tables, and slides broken across cells in a long table, spread over multiple slides, or split between a figure and its discussion. Top-$k$ chunk retrieval treats each fragment independently and cannot represent how evidence connects. We introduce FLOWREADER, which reframes evidence assembly as a min-cost flow problem on a multimodal node graph: a single scoring vector $h$ controls source selection (via MMR), sink selection (via a length-aware answerability proxy), and the costs and capacities of every edge. The optimal flow is decomposed into candidate evidence paths, a compact non-redundant subset is selected by entropy-regularized replicator dynamics, and parallel VLM workers under a dual-process gate produce the answer with a single System-2 refinement pass triggered when answer consistency is low or the routed flow is strained. On VisDoMBench, FLOWREADER is best on the two subsets dominated by fragmented evidence PaperTab ($58.40$, $+1.30$ over G^{2}-Reader) and SlideVQA ($72.93$, $+0.62$) and competitive on SPIQA, FetaTab, and SciGraphQA. Macro-averaged across all five subsets, FLOWREADER ($65.47$) is within $0.74$ of the strongest baseline (G^{2}-Reader, $66.21$). Overall, these results show that min-cost flow performs well on fragmented multimodal evidence, where top-$k$ retrieval fails. It also provides a unified way to control scoring, routing, selection, and adaptive compute together.

</details>


### [89] [Bradley-Terry Rankings for Recommender Systems Across Dataset Taxonomies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07492)
*Ekaterina Grishina,Stepan Kuznetsov,Askar Tsyganov,Ilya Ivanov,Daria Korovaitceva,Margarita Rusanova,Uliana Parkina,Alexander Derevyagin,Evgeny Frolov,Sergey Samsonov,Anton Lysenko*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于 Bradley-Terry (BT) 模型的推荐算法排名方法论，通过在 89 个数据集上对 14 种推荐算法进行基准测试，构建了概率化的算法排名框架。作者还提出了"传递三元组"度量来评估排名一致性，并引入了 BT 树和协变量调整的 BT 模型，以实现无需实际运行模型即可在未见数据集上预测算法排名的数据集感知基线选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统领域算法众多，不同算法在不同类型数据集（稀疏性、顺序结构、规模等）上表现差异显著。传统的朴素聚合方法（如对 NDCG 取平均）可能产生误导性排名，且缺乏理论支撑和缺失数据鲁棒性。此外，研究者和实践者难以在没有实际运行所有候选算法的情况下为新数据集选择合适的基线方法。这些问题凸显了对原则性、数据驱动的算法比较方法论的需求。

Method: 1. **BT 模型基础**：将推荐算法视为"参赛者"，数据集视为"比赛"，算法 i 在某数据集上指标优于算法 j 即为获胜一次，构建胜率矩阵 W。处理平局时使用指标均值±标准差的重叠区间判断。2. **参数估计方法**：采用贝叶斯 BT 模型（带 Metropolis-Hastings MCMC 采样，可获得置信区间）作为主方法，辅以 Zermelo 迭代（MLE）和 Rank Centrality（谱方法）进行比较。3. **Plackett-Luce 扩展**：将成对比较扩展到列表式比较，使用 EM 算法或 Gibbs 采样进行参数估计。4. **传递三元组度量**：提出基于三元组传递性的排名一致性度量，能处理缺失数据情况。5. **协变量调整 BT**：将数据集特征作为协变量，通过带 L1 惩罚的融合正则化项学习算法在不同数据集上的动态强度。6. **BT 树**：通过递归划分自动发现具有不同排名的数据集子群，提供可解释的、数据驱动的分组方案。

Result: 1. **排名质量与稳定性**：BT 和 PL 模型在所有数据集及长历史/稀疏子集上的传递三元组比例和 Kendall's τ 均显著优于 Mean 和 Sum 聚合方法；当缺失数据比例增加时，BT/PL 的排名权重保持稳定，而 Sum/Mean 排名剧烈波动。2. **数据集特征影响排名**：排名随数据集特征显著变化。SASRec/GASATF 在顺序数据集上排名第 1-2，但在非顺序数据集上跌至第 10-11 位；ALS 在非顺序数据上从第 11 升至第 2；长历史数据有利 Seq-KNN/GASATF，短历史则利 LightGCN。3. **不同 BT 估计器一致性**：Zermelo、贝叶斯 BT 和 Rank Centrality 在完整胜率矩阵下收敛到相同权重和排名。4. **未见数据集预测**：在 79/10 训练-留出划分下，协变量调整 BT 的 top-1 命中率（0.28）和 top-2 命中率（0.62）均优于简单 BT（0.24/0.58）；在更困难的 59/30 划分下优势更明显（top-1: 0.24 vs 0.15）。5. **全局排名实用性**：简单的全局 BT 排名在 top-5 重叠度（3.32）上与协变量方法（3.42）相当，证实其可作为基线选择的实用工具。
